# हिन्दी — Hindi / موطأ مالك — Muwatta of Imam Malik

Edition: `deenhindi-web-20261112` · Narrations 1301–1325 of 1836.

[Edition index](README.md) · [Previous](page-0052.md) · [Next](page-0054.md)

## Narration 1301

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1315)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 31</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ سَعِيدِ بْنِ الْمُسَيَّبِ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم نَهَى عَنِ الْمُزَابَنَةِ وَالْمُحَاقَلَةِ وَالْمُزَابَنَةُ اشْتِرَاءُ الثَّمَرِ بِالتَّمْرِ وَالْمُحَاقَلَةُ اشْتِرَاءُ الزَّرْعِ بِالْحِنْطَةِ وَاسْتِكْرَاءُ الأَرْضِ بِالْحِنْطَةِ ‏.‏ قَالَ ابْنُ شِهَابٍ فَسَأَلْتُ سَعِيدَ بْنَ الْمُسَيَّبِ عَنِ اسْتِكْرَاءِ الأَرْضِ بِالذَّهَبِ وَالْوَرِقِ فَقَالَ لاَ بَأْسَ بِذَلِكَ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ نَهَى رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم عَنِ الْمُزَابَنَةِ وَتَفْسِيرُ الْمُزَابَنَةِ أَنَّ كُلَّ شَىْءٍ مِنَ الْجِزَافِ الَّذِي لاَ يُعْلَمُ كَيْلُهُ وَلاَ وَزْنُهُ وَلاَ عَدَدُهُ ابْتِيعَ بِشَىْءٍ مُسَمًّى مِنَ الْكَيْلِ أَوِ الْوَزْنِ أَوِ الْعَدَدِ وَذَلِكَ أَنْ يَقُولَ الرَّجُلُ لِلرَّجُلِ يَكُونُ لَهُ الطَّعَامُ الْمُصَبَّرُ الَّذِي لاَ يُعْلَمُ كَيْلُهُ مِنَ الْحِنْطَةِ أَوِ التَّمْرِ أَوْ مَا أَشْبَهَ ذَلِكَ مِنَ الأَطْعِمَةِ أَوْ يَكُونُ لِلرَّجُلِ السِّلْعَةُ مِنَ الْحِنْطَةِ أَوِ النَّوَى أَوِ الْقَضْبِ أَوِ الْعُصْفُرِ أَوِ الْكُرْسُفِ أَوِ الْكَتَّانِ أَوِ الْقَزِّ أَوْ مَا أَشْبَهَ ذَلِكَ مِنَ السِّلَعِ لاَ يُعْلَمُ كَيْلُ شَىْءٍ مِنْ ذَلِكَ وَلاَ وَزْنُهُ وَلاَ عَدَدُهُ فَيَقُولُ الرَّجُلُ لِرَبِّ تِلْكَ السِّلْعَةِ كِلْ سِلْعَتَكَ هَذِهِ أَوْ مُرْ مَنْ يَكِيلُهَا أَوْ زِنْ مِنْ ذَلِكَ مَا يُوزَنُ أَوْ عُدَّ مِنْ ذَلِكَ مَا كَانَ يُعَدُّ فَمَا نَقَصَ عَنْ كَيْلِ كَذَا وَكَذَا صَاعًا - لِتَسْمِيَةٍ يُسَمِّيهَا - أَوْ وَزْنِ كَذَا وَكَذَا رِطْلاً أَوْ عَدَدِ كَذَا وَكَذَا فَمَا نَقَصَ مِنْ ذَلِكَ فَعَلَىَّ غُرْمُهُ لَكَ حَتَّى أُوفِيَكَ تِلْكَ التَّسْمِيَةَ فَمَا زَادَ عَلَى تِلْكَ التَّسْمِيَةِ فَهُوَ لِي أَضْمَنُ مَا نَقَصَ مِنْ ذَلِكَ عَلَى أَنْ يَكُونَ لِي مَا زَادَ ‏.‏ فَلَيْسَ ذَلِكَ بَيْعًا وَلَكِنَّهُ الْمُخَاطَرَةُ وَالْغَرَرُ وَالْقِمَارُ يَدْخُلُ هَذَا لأَنَّهُ لَمْ يَشْتَرِ مِنْهُ شَيْئًا بِشَىْءٍ أَخْرَجَهُ وَلَكِنَّهُ ضَمِنَ لَهُ مَا سُمِّيَ مِنْ ذَلِكَ الْكَيْلِ أَوِ الْوَزْنِ أَوِ الْعَدَدِ عَلَى أَنْ يَكُونَ لَهُ مَا زَادَ عَلَى ذَلِكَ فَإِنْ نَقَصَتْ تِلْكَ السِّلْعَةُ عَنْ تِلْكَ التَّسْمِيَةِ أَخَذَ مِنْ مَالِ صَاحِبِهِ مَا نَقَصَ بِغَيْرِ ثَمَنٍ وَلاَ هِبَةٍ طَيِّبَةٍ بِهَا نَفْسُهُ فَهَذَا يُشْبِهُ الْقِمَارَ وَمَا كَانَ مِثْلَ هَذَا مِنَ الأَشْيَاءِ فَذَلِكَ يَدْخُلُهُ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَمِنْ ذَلِكَ أَيْضًا أَنْ يَقُولَ الرَّجُلُ لِلرَّجُلِ لَهُ الثَّوْبُ أَضْمَنُ لَكَ مِنْ ثَوْبِكَ هَذَا كَذَا وَكَذَا ظِهَارَةَ قَلَنْسُوَةٍ قَدْرُ كُلِّ ظِهَارَةٍ كَذَا وَكَذَا - لِشَىْءٍ يُسَمِّيهِ - فَمَا نَقَصَ مِنْ ذَلِكَ فَعَلَىَّ غُرْمُهُ حَتَّى أُوفِيَكَ وَمَا زَادَ فَلِي ‏.‏ أَوْ أَنْ يَقُولَ الرَّجُلُ لِلرَّجُلِ أَضْمَنُ لَكَ مِنْ ثِيَابِكَ هَذِي كَذَا وَكَذَا قَمِيصًا ذَرْعُ كُلِّ قَمِيصٍ كَذَا وَكَذَا فَمَا نَقَصَ مِنْ ذَلِكَ فَعَلَىَّ غُرْمُهُ وَمَا زَادَ عَلَى ذَلِكَ فَلِي ‏.‏ أَوْ أَنْ يَقُولُ الرَّجُلُ لِلرَّجُلِ لَهُ الْجُلُودُ مِنْ جُلُودِ الْبَقَرِ أَوِ الإِبِلِ أُقَطِّعُ جُلُودَكَ هَذِهِ نِعَالاً عَلَى إِمَامٍ يُرِيهِ إِيَّاهُ ‏.‏ فَمَا نَقَصَ مِنْ مِائَةِ زَوْجٍ فَعَلَىَّ غُرْمُهُ وَمَا زَادَ فَهُوَ لِي بِمَا ضَمِنْتُ لَكَ ‏.‏ وَمِمَّا يُشْبِهُ ذَلِكَ أَنْ يَقُولَ الرَّجُلُ لِلرَّجُلِ عِنْدَهُ حَبُّ الْبَانِ اعْصُرْ حَبَّكَ هَذَا فَمَا نَقَصَ مِنْ كَذَا وَكَذَا رِطْلاً فَعَلَىَّ أَنْ أُعْطِيَكَهُ وَمَا زَادَ فَهُوَ لِي ‏.‏ فَهَذَا كُلُّهُ وَمَا أَشْبَهَهُ مِنَ الأَشْيَاءِ أَوْ ضَارَعَهُ مِنَ الْمُزَابَنَةِ الَّتِي لاَ تَصْلُحُ وَلاَ تَجُوزُ ‏.‏ وَكَذَلِكَ - أَيْضًا - إِذَا قَالَ الرَّجُلُ لِلرَّجُلِ لَهُ الْخَبَطُ أَوِ النَّوَى أَوِ الْكُرْسُفُ أَوِ الْكَتَّانُ أَوِ الْقَضْبُ أَوِ الْعُصْفُرُ أَبْتَاعُ مِنْكَ هَذَا الْخَبَطَ بِكَذَا وَكَذَا صَاعًا مِنْ خَبَطٍ يُخْبَطُ مِثْلَ خَبَطِهِ أَوْ هَذَا النَّوَى بِكَذَا وَكَذَا صَاعًا مِنْ نَوًى مِثْلِهِ وَفِي الْعُصْفُرِ وَالْكُرْسُفِ وَالْكَتَّانِ وَالْقَضْبِ مِثْلَ ذَلِكَ ‏.‏ فَهَذَا كُلُّهُ يَرْجِعُ إِلَى مَا وَصَفْنَا مِنَ الْمُزَابَنَةِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1315]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1315" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Ibn Shihab from Said ibn al- Musayyab that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, forbade muzabana and muhaqala. Muzabana was selling fresh dates for dried dates. Muhaqala was buying unharvested wheat in exchange for threshed wheat and renting land in exchange for wheat. Ibn Shihab added that he had asked Said ibn al-Musayyab about renting land for gold and silver. He said, &quot;There is no harm in it.&quot; Malik said, &quot;The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, forbade muzabana. The explanation of muzabana is that it is buying something whose number, weight and measure is not known with something whose number, weight or measure is known, for instance, if a man has a stack of food whose measure is not known, either of wheat, dates, or whatever food, or the man has goods of wheat, date kernels, herbs, safflower, cotton, flax, silk, and does not know its measure or weight or number and then a buyer approaches him and proposes that he weigh or measure or count the goods, but, before he does, he specifies a certain weight, or measure, or number and guarantees to pay the price for that amount, agreeing that whatever falls short of that amount is a loss against him and whatever is in excess of that amount is a gain for him. That is not a sale. It is taking risks and it is an uncertain transaction. It falls into the category of gambling because he is not buying something from him for something definite which he pays. Everything which resembles this is also forbidden.&quot; Malik said that another example of that was, for instance, a man proposing to another man, &quot;You have cloth. I will guarantee you from this cloth of yours so many hooded cloaks, the measureof each cloak to be such-and-such, (naming a measurement). Whatever loss there is, is against me and I will fulfill you the specified amount and whatever excess there is, is mine.&quot; Or perhaps the man proposed, &quot;I will guarantee you from this cloth of yours so many shirts, the measurement of each shirt to be such-and-such, and whatever loss there is, is against me and I will fulfill the specified amount and whatever excess there is, is mine.&quot; Or perhaps a man proposed to a man who had cattle or camel hides, &quot;I will cut up these hides of yours into sandals on a pattern I will show you. Whatever falls short of a hundred pairs, I will make up its loss and whatever is over is mine because I guaranteed you.&quot; Another example was that a man say to a man who had ben-nuts, &quot;I will press these nuts of yours. Whatever falls short of such-and-such a weight by the pound, I will make it up, and whatever is more than that is mine.&quot; Malik said that all this and whatever else was like it or resembled it was in the category of muzabana, which was neither good nor permitted. It was also the same case for a man to say to a man, who had fodder leaves, date kernels, cotton, flax, herbs or safflower, &quot;I will buy these leaves from you in exchange for such-and-such a sa, (indicating leaves which are pounded like his leaves) . . or these date kernels for such-and-such a sa of kernels like them, and the like of that in the case of safflower, cotton, flax and herbs.&quot; Malik said, &quot;All this is what we have described of muzabana</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1315]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">यहया ने मुझे मालिक से, उन्होंने इब्न शिहाब से, उन्होंने सईद इब्न अल-मुसय्यब से बयान किया के रसूल अल्लाह सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम ने मुज़ाबना और मुहाक़ला से मना फ़रमाया। मुज़ाबना ताज़ा खजूरों को ख़ुश्क खजूरों के बदले बेचना है। मुहाक़ला बग़ैर कटी हुई गंदुम को कटी हुई गंदुम के बदले खरीदना और ज़मीन को गंदुम के बदले किराये पर देना है। इब्न शिहाब ने मज़ीद कहा के उन्होंने सईद इब्न अल-मुसय्यब से सोने और चांदी के बदले ज़मीन किराये पर लेने के बारे में पूछा तो उन्होंने कहा: &#x27;इस में कोई हर्ज नहीं।&#x27; मालिक ने कहा: &#x27;रसूल अल्लाह सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम ने मुज़ाबना से मना फ़रमाया। मुज़ाबना की वज़ाहत यह है के ऐसी चीज़ का सौदा करना जिस का वज़न, पैमाना या तादाद मालूम न हो, उस चीज़ के बदले जिस का वज़न या पैमाना मालूम हो, मसलन किसी के पास अनाज का ढेर हो जिस का वज़न मालूम न हो और वो उसे एक ख़ास मिक़दार कह कर बेच दे।&#x27;</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1315]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">یحییٰ نے مجھے مالک سے، انہوں نے ابن شہاب سے، انہوں نے سعید بن المسیب سے بیان کیا کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے مزابنہ اور محاقلہ سے منع فرمایا۔ مزابنہ تازہ کھجوروں کو خشک کھجوروں کے بدلے بیچنا ہے۔ محاقلہ بغیر کٹی ہوئی گندم کو کٹی ہوئی گندم کے بدلے خریدنا اور زمین کو گندم کے بدلے کرائے پر دینا ہے۔ ابن شہاب نے مزید کہا کہ انہوں نے سعید بن المسیب سے سونے اور چاندی کے بدلے زمین کرائے پر لینے کے بارے میں پوچھا تو انہوں نے کہا: &#x27;اس میں کوئی حرج نہیں۔&#x27; مالک نے کہا: &#x27;رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے مزابنہ سے منع فرمایا۔ مزابنہ کی وضاحت یہ ہے کہ ایسی چیز کا سودا کرنا جس کا وزن، پیمانہ یا تعداد معلوم نہ ہو، اس چیز کے بدلے جس کا وزن یا پیمانہ معلوم ہو، مثلاً کسی کے پاس اناج کا ڈھیر ہو جس کا وزن معلوم نہ ہو اور وہ اسے ایک خاص مقدار کہہ کر بیچ دے۔&#x27;</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1315]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya ne mujhe Malik se, unhone Ibn Shihab se, unhone Saeed ibn al-Musayyab se bayan kiya ke Rasool Allah Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam ne muzabana aur muhaqala se mana farmaya. Muzabana taza khajuron ko khushk khajuron ke badle bechna hai. Muhaqala baghair kati hui gandum ko kati hui gandum ke badle kharidna aur zameen ko gandum ke badle kiraye par dena hai. Ibn Shihab ne mazeed kaha ke unhone Saeed ibn al-Musayyab se sone aur chandi ke badle zameen kiraye par lene ke bare mein pucha to unhone kaha: &#x27;Is mein koi harj nahi.&#x27; Malik ne kaha: &#x27;Rasool Allah Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam ne muzabana se mana farmaya. Muzabana ki wazahat yeh hai ke aisi cheez ka sauda karna jis ka wazan, paimana ya tadaad maloom na ho, us cheez ke badle jis ka wazan ya paimana maloom ho, maslan kisi ke paas anaj ka dher ho jis ka wazan maloom na ho aur woh use ek khaas miqdar keh kar bech de.&#x27;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1315]</p>

## Narration 1302

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1316)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 31</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1316]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1316" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1316</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1316 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ أَمَرَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم السَّعْدَيْنِ أَنْ يَبِيعَا آنِيَةً مِنَ الْمَغَانِمِ مِنْ ذَهَبٍ أَوْ فِضَّةٍ فَبَاعَا كُلَّ ثَلاَثَةٍ بِأَرْبَعَةٍ عَيْنًا أَوْ كُلَّ أَرْبَعَةٍ بِثَلاَثَةٍ عَيْنًا فَقَالَ لَهُمَا رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏&quot;‏ أَرْبَيْتُمَا فَرُدَّا</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1316" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: Muhammad, as explicitly attributed in this complete source field. Exact English field independently read in full. The selected passage is a locally explicit Prophetic report. Surrounding companion, jurist, compiler and explanatory source material remains under Comments. No source wording is altered and no authenticity or translation-equivalence judgment is made.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that Yahya ibn Said said, &quot;The Messenger of Allah, mayAllah bless him and grant him peace, ordered the two Sads to sell a vessel made of either gold or silver from the booty. They either sold each three units of weight for four units of weight of coins or each four units of weight for three units of weight or coins. The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said to them, &#x27;You have taken usury, so return it</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1316]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1316" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1316</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1316 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Malik said, &quot;There is no harm in buying dates from specified trees or a specified orchard or buying milk from specified sheep when the buyer starts to take them as soon as he has payed the price. That is like buying oil from a container. A man buys some of it for a dinar or two and gives his gold and stipulates that it be measured out for him. There is no harm in that. If the container breaks and the oil is wasted, the buyer has his gold back and there is no transaction between them.&quot; Malik said, &quot;There is no harm in everything which is taken right away as it is, like fresh milk and fresh picked dates which the buyer can take on a day-to-day basis. If the supply runs out before the buyer has what he has paid for in full, the seller gives him back the portion of the gold that is owed to him, or else the buyer takes other goods from him to the value of what he is owed and which they mutually agree about. The buyer should stay with the seller until he has taken it. It is disapproved of for the seller to leave because the transaction would then come into the forbidden category of a debt for a debt. If a stated time period for payment or delivery enters into the transaction, it is also disapproved. Delay and deferment are not permitted in it, and are only acceptable when it is standard practice on definite terms by which the seller guarantees it to the buyer, but this is not to be from one specific orchard or from any specific ewes.&quot; Malik was asked about a man who bought an orchard from another man in which there were various types of palm-trees - excellent ajwa palms, good kabis palms, adhq palms and othertypes. The seller kept aside from the sale the produce of a certain palm of his choice. Malik said, &quot;That is not good because if he does that, and keeps aside, for instance, dates of the ajwa variety whose yield would be 15 sa, and he picks the dates of the kabis in their place, and the yield of their dates is 10 sa or he picks the ajwa which yield 15 sa and leaves the kabis which yield 10 sa, it is as if he bought the ajwa for the kabis making allowances for their difference of quality. This is the same as if a man dealing with a man who has heaps of dates before him - a heap of 15 sa of ajwa, a heap of 10 sa of kabis, and a heap of 12 sa of cadhq, gives the owner of the dates a dinar to let him choose and take whichever of the heaps he likes.&quot; Malik said, &quot;That is not good.&quot; Malik was asked what a man who bought fresh dates from the owner of an orchard and advanced him a dinar was entitled to if the crop was spoilt. Malik said, &quot;The buyer makes a reckoning with the owner of the orchard and takes what is due to him of the dinar. If the buyer has taken two-thirds of a dinar&#x27;s worth of dates, he gets back the third of a dinar which is owed him. If the buyer has taken three-quarters of a dinar&#x27;s worth of dates, then he gets back the quarter which is owed to him, or they come to a mutual agreement, and the buyer takes what is owed him from his dinar from the owner of the orchard in something else of his choosing. If, for instance, he prefers to take dry dates or some other goods, he takes them according to what is due. If he takes dry dates or some other goods, he should stay with him until he has been paid in full.&quot; Malik said, &quot;This is the same situation as hiring out a specified riding-camel or hiring out a slave tailor, carpenter or some other kind of worker or letting a house and taking payment in advance for the hire of the slave or the rent of the house or camel. Then an accident happens to what has been hired resulting in death or something else. The owner of the camel, slave or house returns what remains of the rent of the camel, the hire of the slave or the rent of the house to the one who advanced him the money, and the owner reckons what will settle that up in full. If, for instance, he has provided half of what the man paid for, he returns the remaining half of what he advanced, or according to whatever amount is due.&quot; Malik said, &quot;Paying in advance for something which is on hand is only good when the buyer takes possession of what he has paid for as soon as he hands over the gold, whether it be slave, camel, or house, or in the case of dates, he starts to pick them as soon as he has paid the money.&quot; It is not good that there be any deferment or credit in such a transaction. Malik said, &quot;An example illustrating what is disapproved of in this situation is that, for instance, a man may say that he will pay someone in advance for the use of his camel to ride in the hajj, and the hajj is still some time off, or he may say something similar to that about a slave or a house. When he does that, he only pays the money in advance on the understanding that if he finds the camel to be sound at the time the hire is due to begin, he will take it by virtue of what he has already paid. If an accident, or death, or something happens to the camel, then he will get his money back and the money he paid in advance will be considered as a loan.&quot; Malik said, &quot;This is distinct from someone who takes immediate possession of what he rents or hires, so that it does not fall into the category of &#x27;uncertainty,&#x27; or disapproved payment in advance. That is following a common practice. An example of that is that a man buys a slave, or slave-girl, and takes possession of them and pays their price. If something happens to them within the period of the year indemnification contract, he takes his gold back from the one from whom he bought it. There is no harm in that. This is the precedent of the sunna in the matter of selling slaves.&quot; Malik said, &quot;Someone who rents a specified slave, or hires a specified camel, for a future date, at which time he will take possession of the camel or slave, has not acted properly because he did not take possession of what he rented or hired, nor is he advancing a loan which the person is responsible to pay back.&quot; Malik said, &quot;The generally agreed on way of doing things among us is that some one who buys some fruit, fresh or dry, should not resell it until he gets full possession of it. He should not barter things of the same type, except hand to hand. Whatever can be made into dry fruit to be stored and eaten, should not be bartered for its own kind, except hand to hand, like for like, when it is the same kind of fruit. In the case of two different kinds of fruit, there is no harm in bartering two of one kind for one of another, hand to hand on the spot. It is not good to set delayed terms. As for produce which is not dried and stored but is eaten fresh like water melon, cucumber, melon, carrots, citron, medlars, pomegranates, and soon, which when dried no longer counts as fruit, and is not a thing which is stored up as is fruit, I think that it is quite proper to barter such things two for one of the same variety hand to hand. If no term enters into it, there is no harm in it.&quot; </p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1316" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

## Narration 1303

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1317)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 31</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ مُوسَى بْنِ أَبِي تَمِيمٍ، عَنْ أَبِي الْحُبَابِ، سَعِيدِ بْنِ يَسَارٍ عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ الدِّينَارُ بِالدِّينَارِ وَالدِّرْهَمُ بِالدِّرْهَمِ لاَ فَضْلَ بَيْنَهُمَا ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1317]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1317" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1317</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1317 ]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Musa ibn Abi Tamim from Abu&#x27;l Hubab Said ibn Yasar from Abu Hurayra that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;A dinar for a dinar, a dirham for a dirham, no excess between the two</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1317]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1317" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 1304

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1318)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 31</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنْ أَبِي سَعِيدٍ الْخُدْرِيِّ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ لاَ تَبِيعُوا الذَّهَبَ بِالذَّهَبِ إِلاَّ مِثْلاً بِمِثْلٍ وَلاَ تُشِفُّوا بَعْضَهَا عَلَى بَعْضٍ وَلاَ تَبِيعُوا الْوَرِقَ بِالْوَرِقِ إِلاَّ مِثْلاً بِمِثْلٍ وَلاَ تُشِفُّوا بَعْضَهَا عَلَى بَعْضٍ وَلاَ تَبِيعُوا مِنْهَا شَيْئًا غَائِبًا بِنَاجِزٍ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1318]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1318" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Nafi from Abu Said al-Khudri that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;Do not sell gold for gold except like for like and do not increase one part over another part. Do not sell silver for silver, except like for like and do not increase one part over another part. Do not sell some of it which is not there for some of it which is</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1318]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1318" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अबू सईद खुदरी (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फरमाया: सोने के बदले में सोना मत बेचो मगर बराबर, ना ज्यादा करो एक दूसरे पर और चांदी के बदले में चांदी मत बेचो मगर बराबर, ना ज्यादा करो एक दूसरे पर और इस में से कुछ नकद वादा पर मत बेचो।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1318]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1318" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ابو سعید خدری سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا مت بیچو سونے کے بدلے میں سونا مگر برابر نہ زیادہ کرو ایک دوسرے پر اور مت بیچو چاندی کے بدلے میں چاندی کے مگر برابر نہ زیادہ کرو ایک دوسرے پر نہ بیچو کچھ اس میں سے نقد وعدہ پر ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1318]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1318" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Saeed Khudri (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Sone ke badle mein sona mat becho magar barabar, na zyada karo ek dusre par aur chandi ke badle mein chandi mat becho magar barabar, na zyada karo ek dusre par aur is mein se kuch naqad waada par mat becho.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1318]</p>

## Narration 1305

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1319)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 31</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1319]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1319" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1319</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1319 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ حُمَيْدِ بْنِ قَيْسٍ الْمَكِّيِّ، عَنْ مُجَاهِدٍ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ كُنْتُ مَعَ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ فَجَاءَهُ صَائِغٌ فَقَالَ لَهُ يَا أَبَا عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ إِنِّي أَصُوغُ الذَّهَبَ ثُمَّ أَبِيعُ الشَّىْءَ مِنْ ذَلِكَ بِأَكْثَرَ مِنْ وَزْنِهِ فَأَسْتَفْضِلُ مِنْ ذَلِكَ قَدْرَ عَمَلِ يَدِي ‏.‏ فَنَهَاهُ عَبْدُ اللَّهِ عَنْ ذَلِكَ فَجَعَلَ الصَّائِغُ يُرَدِّدُ عَلَيْهِ الْمَسْأَلَةَ وَعَبْدُ اللَّهِ يَنْهَاهُ حَتَّى انْتَهَى إِلَى بَابِ الْمَسْجِدِ أَوْ إِلَى دَابَّةٍ يُرِيدُ أَنْ يَرْكَبَهَا ثُمَّ قَالَ عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ عُمَرَ الدِّينَارُ بِالدِّينَارِ وَالدِّرْهَمُ بِالدِّرْهَمِ لاَ فَضْلَ بَيْنَهُمَا هَذَا عَهْدُ نَبِيِّنَا إِلَيْنَا وَعَهْدُنَا إِلَيْكُمْ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1319" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abdullah ibn Umar answering the artisan in Mujahids witnessed account. The complete field supplies the repeated question, prohibition and Ibn Umars final instruction explicitly invoking the Prophets command. Preserve the actual answering Companion.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Humayd ibn Qays al-Makki that Mujahid said, &quot;I was with Abdullah ibn Umar and an artisan came to him and said, &#x27;Abu Abd ar-Rahman - I fashion gold and then sell what I have made for more than its weight. I take an amount equivalent to the work of my hand.&#x27; Abdullah forbade him to do that, so the artisan repeated the question to him, and Abdullah continued to forbid him until he came to the door of the mosque or to an animal that he intended to mount. Then Abdullah ibn Umar said, &#x27;A dinar for a dinar, and a dirham for a dirham. There is no increase between them. This is the command of ourProphet to us and our advice to you</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1319]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1319" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1319</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1319 ]</p>

<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1319]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1319" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1319</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1319 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">मुजाहिद से रिवायत है के मैं अब्दुल्लाह बिन उमर (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) के पास बैठा था इतने में एक सुनार आया और बोला: ऐ अबू अब्दुर्रहमान! मैं सोने का जेवर बनाता हूँ फिर उसके वज़न से ज़्यादा दीनार लेकर उसको बेचता हूँ और ये ज़्यादती अपनी मेहनत के एवज़ में लेता हूँ। अब्दुल्लाह बिन उमर (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) मना करते रहे यहाँ तक के वो मस्जिद के दरवाज़े पर आए या अपने जानवर पर सवार होने लगे, उस वक़्त अब्दुल्लाह बिन उमर (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा: दीनार को दीनार के बदले में और दिरहम को दिरहम के बदले में बेच और ज़्यादती ना ले, यही नसीहत है। हज़रत उस्मान (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फरमाया: एक दीनार को दो दीनार के बदले में ना बेचो, ना एक दिरहम को दो दिरहम के बदले में।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1319" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">مجاہد سے روایت ہے کہ عبداللہ بن عمر کے پاس بیٹھا تھا اتنے میں ایک سنار آیا اور بولا اے ابوعبدالرحمن میں سونے کا زیور بناتا ہوں پھر اس کے وزن سے زیادہ دینار لے کر اس کو بیچتا ہوں اور یہ زیادتی اپنی محنت کے عوض میں لیتا ہوں عبداللہ بن عمر منع کرتے رہے یہاں تک کہ عبداللہ بن عمر مسجد کے دروازے پر آئے یا اپنے جانور پر سوار ہونے کو آئے اس وقت عبداللہ بن عمر نے کہا دینار کو بدلے میں دینار کے اور درہم کو بدلے میں درہم کے بیچ اور زیادتی نہ لے یہی وصیت ہے ۔ حضرت عثمان سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا مت بیچو ایک دینار کو دو دینار کے بدلے میں نہ ایک درہم کو دو درہم کے بدلے میں ۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1319" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Mujahid se riwayat hai ke main Abdullah bin Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ke paas baitha tha itne mein ek sunar aaya aur bola: Ae Abu Abdurrahman! Main sone ka zewar banata hoon phir uske wazan se zyada dinar lekar usko bechta hoon aur ye zyadati apni mehnat ke ewaz mein leta hoon. Abdullah bin Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) mana karte rahe yahan tak ke wo masjid ke darwaze par aaye ya apne janwar par sawar hone lage, us waqt Abdullah bin Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha: Dinar ko dinar ke badle mein aur dirham ko dirham ke badle mein bech aur zyadati na le, yahi nasihat hai. Hazrat Usman (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Ek dinar ko do dinar ke badle mein na becho, na ek dirham ko do dirham ke badle mein.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1319" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1306

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1320)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 31</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ عَنْ جَدِّهِ، مَالِكِ بْنِ أَبِي عَامِرٍ أَنَّ عُثْمَانَ بْنَ عَفَّانَ، قَالَ قَالَ لِي رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏ &quot;‏ لاَ تَبِيعُوا الدِّينَارَ بِالدِّينَارَيْنِ وَلاَ الدِّرْهَمَ بِالدِّرْهَمَيْنِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1320]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1320" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he had heard from his grandfather, Malik ibn Abi Amir that Uthman ibn Affan said, &quot;The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said to me, &#x27;Do not sell a dinar for two dinars nor a dirham for two dirhams</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1320]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1320" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">याह्या ने मुझे मालिक से बयां किया कि उन्होंने अपने दादा मालिक इब्न आबि आमिर से सुना के उथमण इब्न अफ्फान रदिअल्लहु अन्हु ने कहा: &#x27;रसूल अल्लाह सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम ने मुझ से फ़रमाया: एक दीनार के बदले दो दीनार न बेचो और न ही एक दिरहम के बदले दो दिरहैम.&#x27;</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1320]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1320" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">یحییٰ نے مجھے مالک سے بیان کیا کہ انہوں نے اپنے دادا مالک بن ابی عامر سے سنا کہ عثمان بن عفان رضی اللہ عنہ نے کہا: &#x27;رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے مجھ سے فرمایا: ایک دینار کے بدلے دو دینار نہ بیچو اور نہ ہی ایک درہم کے بدلے دو درہم۔&#x27;</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1320]</p>

## Narration 1307

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1321)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 31</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ زَيْدِ بْنِ أَسْلَمَ، عَنْ عَطَاءِ بْنِ يَسَارٍ، أَنَّ مُعَاوِيَةَ بْنَ أَبِي سُفْيَانَ، بَاعَ سِقَايَةً مِنْ ذَهَبٍ أَوْ وَرِقٍ بِأَكْثَرَ مِنْ وَزْنِهَا فَقَالَ أَبُو الدَّرْدَاءِ سَمِعْتُ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم يَنْهَى عَنْ مِثْلِ هَذَا إِلاَّ مِثْلاً بِمِثْلٍ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ لَهُ مُعَاوِيَةُ مَا أَرَى بِمِثْلِ هَذَا بَأْسًا ‏.‏ فَقَالَ أَبُو الدَّرْدَاءِ مَنْ يَعْذِرُنِي مِنْ مُعَاوِيَةَ أَنَا أُخْبِرُهُ عَنْ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم وَيُخْبِرُنِي عَنْ رَأْيِهِ لاَ أُسَاكِنُكَ بِأَرْضٍ أَنْتَ بِهَا ‏.‏ ثُمَّ قَدِمَ أَبُو الدَّرْدَاءِ عَلَى عُمَرَ بْنِ الْخَطَّابِ فَذَكَرَ ذَلِكَ لَهُ فَكَتَبَ عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ إِلَى مُعَاوِيَةَ أَنْ لاَ تَبِيعَ ذَلِكَ إِلاَّ مِثْلاً بِمِثْلٍ وَزْنًا بِوَزْنٍ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1321]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1321" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Zayd ibn Aslam from Ata ibn Yasar that Muawiya ibn Abi Sufyan sold a gold or silver drinking- vessel for more than its weight. Abu&#x27;dDarda said, &quot;I heard the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, forbidding such sales except like for like.&quot; Muawiya said to him, &quot;I don&#x27;t see any harm in it.&quot; Abu&#x27;d-Darda said to him, &quot;Who will excuse me from Muawiya? I tell him something from the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, and he gives me his own opinion! I will not live in the same land as you!&quot; Then Abu&#x27;d-Darda went to Umar ibn al-Khattab and mentioned that to him. Umar ibn al-Khattab therefore wrote to Muawiya, &quot;Do not sell it except like for like, weight for weight</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1321]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1321" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अता बिन यासर से रिवायत है के मुआविया बिन अबी सुफियान (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने एक बर्तन पानी पीने का सोने या चांदी का उसके वज़न से ज़्यादा सोने या चांदी के बदले में बेचा, तो अबू दरदा (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने उन से कहा: मैंने रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से सुना, आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) इस से मना करते थे मगर बराबर-बराबर बेचना दुरुस्त रखते थे। मुआविया ने कहा: मेरे नज़दीक कुछ क़बाहत नहीं है। अबू दरदा ने कहा: भला कौन मेरे उज़्र क़बूल करेगा अगर मैं मुआविया को इस बदले दूँ? मैं तो उन से रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) की हदीस बयान करता हूँ और वो मुझ से अपनी राय बयान करते हैं, अब मैं तुम्हारे मुल्क में ना रहूँगा। फिर अबू दरदा मदीना में हज़रत उमर (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) के पास आए और उन से ये क़िस्सा बयान किया। हज़रत उमर ने मुआविया को लिखा के ऐसी बेच ना करें मगर बराबर तौल कर।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1321]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1321" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">عطاء بن یسار سے روایت ہے کہ معاویہ بن ابی سفیان نے ایک برتن پانی پینے کا سونے یا چاندی کا اس کے وزن سے زیادہ سونے یا چاندی کے بدلے میں بیچا تو ابوالدردا نے ان سے کہا میں نے رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم سے سنا آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم اس سے منع کرتے تھے مگر برابر برابر بیچنا درست رکھتے تھے معاویہ نے کہا میرے نزدیک کچھ قباحت نہیں ہے ابوالدردا نے کہا بھلا کان میرے عذر قبول کرے گا اگر میں معاویہ کو اس بدلہ دوں میں تو ان سے رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کی حدیث بیان کرتا ہوں اور وہ مجھ سے اپنی رائے بیان کرتے ہیں اب میں تمہارے ملک میں نہ رہوں گا پھر ابودردا مدینہ میں حضرت عمر کے پاس آئے اور ان سے یہ قصہ بیان کیا حضرت عمر نے معاویہ کو لکھا کہ ایسی بیع نہ کریں مگر برابر تول کر۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1321]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1321" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Ata bin Yasar se riwayat hai ke Muawiyah bin Abi Sufyan (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne ek bartan pani peene ka sone ya chandi ka uske wazan se zyada sone ya chandi ke badle mein becha, to Abu Darda (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne un se kaha: Maine Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se suna, aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) is se mana karte the magar barabar-barabar bechna durust rakhte the. Muawiyah ne kaha: Mere nazdeek kuch qabahat nahi hai. Abu Darda ne kaha: Bhala kaun mere uzr qabool karega agar main Muawiyah ko is badle dun? Main to un se Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ki hadith bayan karta hun aur wo mujh se apni raaye bayan karte hain, ab main tumhare mulk mein na rahunga. Phir Abu Darda Madina mein Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ke paas aaye aur un se ye qissa bayan kiya. Hazrat Umar ne Muawiyah ko likha ke aisi bech na karen magar barabar taul kar.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1321]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1321" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 1308

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1322)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 31</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ، أَنَّ عُمَرَ بْنَ الْخَطَّابِ، قَالَ لاَ تَبِيعُوا الذَّهَبَ بِالذَّهَبِ إِلاَّ مِثْلاً بِمِثْلٍ وَلاَ تُشِفُّوا بَعْضَهَا عَلَى بَعْضٍ وَلاَ تَبِيعُوا الْوَرِقَ بِالْوَرِقِ إِلاَّ مِثْلاً بِمِثْلٍ وَلاَ تُشِفُّوا بَعْضَهَا عَلَى بَعْضٍ وَلاَ تَبِيعُوا الْوَرِقَ بِالذَّهَبِ أَحَدُهُمَا غَائِبٌ وَالآخَرُ نَاجِزٌ وَإِنِ اسْتَنْظَرَكَ إِلَى أَنْ يَلِجَ بَيْتَهُ فَلاَ تُنْظِرْهُ إِنِّي أَخَافُ عَلَيْكُمُ الرَّمَاءَ وَالرَّمَاءُ هُوَ الرِّبَا ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1322" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar ibn al-Khattab (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Nafi from Abdullah ibn Umar that Umar ibn al-Khattab said, &quot;Do not sell gold for gold except like for like, and do not increase one part over another part. Do not sell silver for silver except like for like, and do not increase one part over another part. Do not sell silver for gold, one of them at hand and the other to be given later. If someone seeks to delay paying you until he has been to his house, do not leave him. I fear rama for you.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1322]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1322" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">हज़रत उमर बिन खत्ताब (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने फरमाया: मत बेचो सोने को बदले में सोने के मगर बराबर-बराबर, ना ज़्यादा करो एक को दूसरे पर, और ना बेचो चांदी के बदले में चांदी के मगर बराबर, ना ज़्यादा करो एक को दूसरे पर। और ना बेचो चांदी को बदले में सोने के इस तरह पर के एक नक़द हो और दूसरा वादे पर, बल्के तुझ से अगर इतनी मोहलत चाहे के अपने घर में से हो कर आए तो इतनी भी इजाज़त मत दे, मैं ख़ौफ़ करता हूँ तुम्हारे ऊपर सूद का।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1322" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حضرت عمر بن خطاب نے فرمایا مت بیچو سونے کو بدلے میں سونے کے مگر برابر برابر نہ زیادہ کرو ایک کو دوسرے پر اور نہ بیچو چاندی کے بدے میں چاندی کے مگر برابر نہ زیادہ کرو ایک کو دوسرے پر اور نہ بیچو چاندی کو بدلے میں سونے کے اس طرح پر کہ ایک نقد ہو اور دوسرا وعدے پر بلکہ تجھ سے اگر اتنی مہلت چاہے کہ اپنے گھر میں سے ہو کر آئے تو اتنی بھی اجازت مت دے میں خوف کرتا ہوں تمہارے اوپر سود کا ۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1322" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Hazrat Umar bin Khattab (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne farmaya: Mat becho sone ko badle mein sone ke magar barabar-barabar, na zyada karo ek ko dusre par, aur na becho chandi ke badle mein chandi ke magar barabar, na zyada karo ek ko dusre par. Aur na becho chandi ko badle mein sone ke is tarah par ke ek naqd ho aur dusra wade par, balkeh tujh se agar itni mohlat chahe ke apne ghar mein se ho kar aaye to itni bhi ijazat mat de, main khauf karta hun tumhare upar sood ka.</p>

## Narration 1309

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1323)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 31</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1323]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1323" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1323</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1323 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ دِينَارٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ، أَنَّ عُمَرَ بْنَ الْخَطَّابِ، قَالَ لاَ تَبِيعُوا الذَّهَبَ بِالذَّهَبِ إِلاَّ مِثْلاً بِمِثْلٍ وَلاَ تُشِفُّوا بَعْضَهَا عَلَى بَعْضٍ وَلاَ تَبِيعُوا الْوَرِقَ بِالْوَرِقِ إِلاَّ مِثْلاً بِمِثْلٍ وَلاَ تُشِفُّوا بَعْضَهَا عَلَى بَعْضٍ وَلاَ تَبِيعُوا شَيْئًا مِنْهَا غَائِبًا بِنَاجِزٍ ‏.‏ وَإِنِ اسْتَنْظَرَكَ إِلَى أَنْ يَلِجَ بَيْتَهُ فَلاَ تُنْظِرْهُ إِنِّي أَخَافُ عَلَيْكُمُ الرَّمَاءَ وَالرَّمَاءُ هُوَ الرِّبَا ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1323" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar ibn al-Khattab (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Abdullah ibn Dinar from Abdullah ibn Umar that Umar ibn al-Khattab said, &quot;Do not sell gold for gold except like for like. Do not increase part of it over another part. Do not sell silver for silver except like for like, and do not increase part of it over another part. Do not sell some of it which is there for some of it which is not. If someone asks you to wait for payment until he has been to his house, do not leave him. I fear rama for you.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1323]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1323" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1323 ]</p>

<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Rama is usury</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">हज़रत उमर बिन खत्ताब (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने फरमाया: बेचो सोने को सोने के मगर बराबर-बराबर, ना ज़्यादा करो एक को दूसरे पर। और ना बेचो एक को दूसरे पर (ना-बराबर), और ना बेचो चांदी को बदले में सोने के इस तरह पर के एक नक़द हो दूसरा वादे पर, बल्के तुझ से अगर इतनी मोहलत चाहे के अपने घर में से हो कर आए तो इतनी भी इजाज़त मत दे, मैं ख़ौफ़ करता हूँ तुम्हारे ऊपर सूद का। हज़रत उमर ने कहा: एक दीनार बदले में एक दीनार के, चाहे एक दिरहम बदले में एक दिरहम के और एक सा&#x27;अ बदले में एक सा&#x27;अ के, और ना बेचो नक़द बदले में वादे के।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1323" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<p dir="rtl">حضرت عمر بن خطاب نے فرمایا بیچو سونے کو سونے کے مگر برابر برابر نہ زیادہ کرو ایک کو دوسرے پر اور نہ بیچو ایک کو دوسرے پر اور نہ بیچو چاندی کو بدلے میں سونے کے اس طرح پر کہ ایک نقد ہو دوسرا وعدہ پر بلکہ تجھ سے اگر اتنی مہلت چاہے کہ اپنے گھر میں سے ہو کر آئے تو اتنی بھی اجازت مت دے میں خوف کرتا ہوں تمہارے اوپر سود کا ۔ حضرت عمر نے کہا ایک دینار بدلے میں ایک دینار کے چاہے ایک درہم بدلے میں ایک درہم کے اور ایک صاع بدلے میں ایک صاع کے اور نہ بیچو نقد بدلے میں وعدے کے ۔</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Hazrat Umar bin Khattab (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne farmaya: Becho sone ko sone ke magar barabar-barabar, na zyada karo ek ko dusre par. Aur na becho ek ko dusre par (na-barabar), aur na becho chandi ko badle mein sone ke is tarah par ke ek naqd ho dusra wade par, balkeh tujh se agar itni mohlat chahe ke apne ghar mein se ho kar aaye to itni bhi ijazat mat de, main khauf karta hun tumhare upar sood ka. Hazrat Umar ne kaha: Ek dinar badle mein ek dinar ke, chahe ek dirham badle mein ek dirham ke aur ek saa badle mein ek saa ke, aur na becho naqd badle mein wade ke.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1323" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 31</strong></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1324]</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1324</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1324 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ عَنِ الْقَاسِمِ بْنِ مُحَمَّدٍ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ قَالَ عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ الدِّينَارُ بِالدِّينَارِ وَالدِّرْهَمُ بِالدِّرْهَمِ وَالصَّاعُ بِالصَّاعِ وَلاَ يُبَاعُ كَالِئٌ بِنَاجِزٍ ‏.‏</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar ibn al-Khattab (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he had heard that al-Oasim ibn Muhammad said, &#x27;&#x27;Umar ibn al-Khattab said, &#x27;A dinar for a dinar, and a dirham for adirham, and a sa for a sa. Something to be collected later is not to be sold for something at hand</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1324]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1324" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">यहया ने मुझे मालिक से बयान किया के उन्होंने सुना के अल-क़ासिम इब्न मुहम्मद ने कहा के उमर इब्न अल-ख़त्ताब ने फ़रमाया: &#x27;एक दीनार का बदला एक दीनार, एक दिरहम का बदला एक दिरहम, और एक सा का बदला एक सा है। जो चीज़ बाद में वसूल करनी हो उसे हाज़िर चीज़ के बदले नहीं बेचा जा सकता।&#x27;</p>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ أَبِي الزِّنَادِ، أَنَّهُ سَمِعَ سَعِيدَ بْنَ الْمُسَيَّبِ، يَقُولُ لاَ رِبًا إِلاَّ فِي ذَهَبٍ أَوْ فِضَّةٍ أَوْ مَا يُكَالُ أَوْ يُوزَنُ بِمَا يُؤْكَلُ أَوْ يُشْرَبُ ‏.‏</p>

<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: Said ibn al-Musayyab (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that Abu&#x27;z-Zinad heard Said ibn al-Musayyab say, &quot;There is usury only in gold or silver or what is weighed or measured of what is eaten or drunk</p>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، أَنَّهُ سَمِعَ سَعِيدَ بْنَ الْمُسَيَّبِ، يَقُولُ قَطْعُ الذَّهَبِ وَالْوَرِقِ مِنَ الْفَسَادِ فِي الأَرْضِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَلاَ بَأْسَ أَنْ يَشْتَرِيَ الرَّجُلُ الذَّهَبَ بِالْفِضَّةِ وَالْفِضَّةَ بِالذَّهَبِ جِزَافًا إِذَا كَانَ تِبْرًا أَوْ حَلْيًا قَدْ صِيغَ فَأَمَّا الدَّرَاهِمُ الْمَعْدُودَةُ وَالدَّنَانِيرُ الْمَعْدُودَةُ فَلاَ يَنْبَغِي لأَحَدٍ أَنْ يَشْتَرِيَ ذَلِكَ جِزَافًا حَتَّى يُعْلَمَ وَيُعَدَّ فَإِنِ اشْتُرِيَ ذَلِكَ جِزَافًا فَإِنَّمَا يُرَادُ بِهِ الْغَرَرُ حِينَ يُتْرَكُ عَدُّهُ وَيُشْتَرَى جِزَافًا وَلَيْسَ هَذَا مِنْ بُيُوعِ الْمُسْلِمِينَ فَأَمَّا مَا كَانَ يُوزَنُ مِنَ التِّبْرِ وَالْحَلْىِ فَلاَ بَأْسَ أَنْ يُبَاعَ ذَلِكَ جِزَافًا وَإِنَّمَا ابْتِيَاعُ ذَلِكَ جِزَافًا كَهَيْئَةِ الْحِنْطَةِ وَالتَّمْرِ وَنَحْوِهِمَا مِنَ الأَطْعِمَةِ الَّتِي تُبَاعُ جِزَافًا وَمِثْلُهَا يُكَالُ فَلَيْسَ بِابْتِيَاعِ ذَلِكَ جِزَافًا بَأْسٌ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ مَنِ اشْتَرَى مُصْحَفًا أَوْ سَيْفًا أَوْ خَاتَمًا وَفِي شَىْءٍ مِنْ ذَلِكَ ذَهَبٌ أَوْ فِضَّةٌ بِدَنَانِيرَ أَوْ دَرَاهِمَ فَإِنَّ مَا اشْتُرِيَ مِنْ ذَلِكَ وَفِيهِ الذَّهَبُ بِدَنَانِيرَ فَإِنَّهُ يُنْظَرُ إِلَى قِيمَتِهِ فَإِنْ كَانَتْ قِيمَةُ ذَلِكَ الثُّلُثَيْنِ وَقِيمَةُ مَا فِيهِ مِنَ الذَّهَبِ الثُّلُثَ فَذَلِكَ جَائِزٌ لاَ بَأْسَ بِهِ إِذَا كَانَ ذَلِكَ يَدًا بِيَدٍ وَلاَ يَكُونُ فِيهِ تَأْخِيرٌ وَمَا اشْتُرِيَ مِنْ ذَلِكَ بِالْوَرِقِ مِمَّا فِيهِ الْوَرِقُ نُظِرَ إِلَى قِيمَتِهِ فَإِنْ كَانَ قِيمَةُ ذَلِكَ الثُّلُثَيْنِ وَقِيمَةُ مَا فِيهِ مِنَ الْوَرِقِ الثُّلُثَ فَذَلِكَ جَائِزٌ لاَ بَأْسَ بِهِ إِذَا كَانَ ذَلِكَ يَدًا بِيَدٍ وَلَمْ يَزَلْ ذَلِكَ مِنْ أَمْرِ النَّاسِ عِنْدَنَا ‏.‏</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: Said ibn al-Musayyab (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that Yahya ibn Said heard Said ibn al-Musayyab say, &quot;Keeping gold and silver out of circulation is part of working corruption in the land.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1326]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1326" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1326</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1326 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said, &quot;There is no harm in buying gold with silver or silver with gold without measuring if it is unminted or a piece of jewellery which has been made. Counted dirhams and counted dinars should not be bought without reckoning until they are known and counted. To abandon number and buy them at random would only be to speculate. That is not part of the business transactions of Muslims. As for what is weighed of unminted objects and jewellery, there is no harm in buying such things without measuring. To buy them without measuring is like buying wheat, dried dates, and such food-stuffs, which are sold without measuring, even though things like them are measured &quot; Malik spoke about buying a Qur&#x27;an, a sword or a signet ring which had some gold or silver work on it with dinars or dirhams. He said, &quot;The value of the object bought with dinars, which has gold in it is looked at. If the value of the gold is up to one-third of the price, it is permitted and there is no harm in it if the sale is hand to hand and there is no deferment in it. When something is bought with silver which has silver in it, the value is looked at. If the value of the silver is one- third, it is permitted and there is no harm in it if the sale is hand to hand. That is still the way of doing things among us</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1326" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1326 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">सईद बिन मुसैय्यब (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) कहते थे: रुपया अशरफ़ी का कांटा गोया मुल्क में फ़साद करना है। कहा इमाम मालिक (रहमतुल्लाह अलैह) ने: अगर सोने को चांदी के बदले में या चांदी को सोने के बदले में ढेर लगा कर खरीदे तो कुछ क़बाहत नहीं है जब वो डल्ली हों या ज़ेवर हों। लेकिन रुपये अशरफ़ी का खरीदना ब ग़ैर गिने हुए जाइज़ नहीं बल्के इस में धोका है और मुसलमानों के दस्तूर के ख़िलाफ़ है। लेकिन सोने चांदी का ड़ुल्ला या ज़ेवर जो तौल के बिकता है, उसको अटकल से खरीदना जैसे गेहूं या खजूर वग़ैरा को खरीदते हैं, बुरा नहीं है। कहा मालिक ने: जो शख़्स कलाम-ए-मजीद या तलवार या अंगूठी जिस में सोना या चांदी लगा हो, रुपये अशरफ़ी के बदले में खरीद करे तो देखेंगे, अगर उन चीज़ों में सोना लगा हुआ है और अशरफ़ियों के बदले में उसको खरीद किया और उस चीज़ की क़ीमत दो-सुलस से कम नहीं है और जिस क़दर सोना उस में लगा हुआ है उसकी क़ीमत एक-सुलस से ज़्यादा नहीं है तो दुरुस्त है जब नक़दन-नक़द हो। इसी तरह अगर चांदी लगी हुई है और रुपयों के बदले में खरीद किया तब भी यही हुक्म है।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1326" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">سعید بن مسیب کہتے تھے روپیہ اشرفی کا کانٹا گویا ملک میں فساد کرنا ہے ۔ کہا امام مالک رحمة اللہ علیہ نے اگر سونے کو چاندی کے بدلے میں یا چاندی کو سونے کے بدلے میں ڈھیر لگا کر خریدے تو کچھ قباحت نہیں ہے جب وہ ڈلی ہوں یا زیور ہوں لیکن روپے اشرفی کا خریدنا بغیر گنے ہوئے جائز نہیں بلکہ اس میں دھوکا ہے اور مسلمانوں کے دستور کے خلاف ہے لیکن سونے چاندی کا ڈلا یا زیور جو تل کے بکتا ہے اس کو اٹکل سے خریدنا جیسے گیہوں یا کھجور وغیرہ کو خریدتے ہیں برا نہیں ہے۔ کہا مالک نے جو شخص کلام مجید یا تلوار یا انگوٹھی جس میں سونا یا چاندی لگا ہو روپے اشرفی کے بدلے میں خرید کرے تو دیکھیں گے اگر ان چیزوں میں سونا لگا ہوا ہے اور اشرفیوں کے بدلے میں اس کو خرید کیا اور اس چیز کی قیمت دو ثلث سے کم نہیں ہے اور جس قدر سونا اس میں لگا ہوا ہے اس کی قیمت ایک ثلث سے زیادہ نہیں ہے تو درست ہے جب نقدا نقد ہو اسی طرح اگر چاندی لگی ہوئی ہے اور روپیوں کے بدلے میں خرید کیا تب بھی یہی حکم ہے۔</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Saeed bin Musayyib (Radi Allahu Anhu) kehte the: Rupiya ashrafi ka kanta goya mulk mein fasad karna hai. Kaha Imam Malik (Rahmatullah Alaih) ne: Agar sone ko chandi ke badle mein ya chandi ko sone ke badle mein dher laga kar khareede to kuch qabahat nahi hai jab wo dalli hon ya zevar hon. Lekin rupaye ashrafi ka khareedna baghair gine hue jaiz nahi balkeh is mein dhoka hai aur musalmanon ke dastoor ke khilaf hai. Lekin sone chandi ka dulla ya zevar jo taul ke bikta hai, usko atkal se khareedna jaise gehoon ya khajoor waghaira ko khareedte hain, bura nahi hai. Kaha Malik ne: Jo shakhs kalam-e-majeed ya talwar ya angoothi jis mein sona ya chandi laga ho, rupaye ashrafi ke badle mein khareed kare to dekhenge, agar un cheezon mein sona laga hua hai aur ashrafiyon ke badle mein usko khareed kiya aur us cheez ki qeemat do-sulth se kam nahi hai aur jis qadar sona us mein laga hua hai uski qeemat ek-sulth se zyada nahi hai to durust hai jab naqdan-naqd ho. Isi tarah agar chandi lagi hui hai aur rupayon ke badle mein khareed kiya tab bhi yahi hukm hai.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1326" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1313

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1327)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 31</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ مَالِكِ بْنِ أَوْسِ بْنِ الْحَدَثَانِ النَّصْرِيِّ، أَنَّهُ الْتَمَسَ صَرْفًا بِمِائَةِ دِينَارٍ قَالَ فَدَعَانِي طَلْحَةُ بْنُ عُبَيْدِ اللَّهِ فَتَرَاوَضْنَا حَتَّى اصْطَرَفَ مِنِّي وَأَخَذَ الذَّهَبَ يُقَلِّبُهَا فِي يَدِهِ ثُمَّ قَالَ حَتَّى يَأْتِيَنِي خَازِنِي مِنَ الْغَابَةِ ‏.‏ وَعُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ يَسْمَعُ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ عُمَرُ وَاللَّهِ لاَ تُفَارِقْهُ حَتَّى تَأْخُذَ مِنْهُ - ثُمَّ قَالَ - قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏ &quot;‏ الذَّهَبُ بِالْوَرِقِ رِبًا إِلاَّ هَاءَ وَهَاءَ وَالْبُرُّ بِالْبُرِّ رِبًا إِلاَّ هَاءَ وَهَاءَ وَالتَّمْرُ بِالتَّمْرِ رِبًا إِلاَّ هَاءَ وَهَاءَ وَالشَّعِيرُ بِالشَّعِيرِ رِبًا إِلاَّ هَاءَ وَهَاءَ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1327]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1327" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Ibn Shihab from Malik ibn Aus ibn al-Hadathan an-Nasri that one time he asked to exchange 100 dinars. He said, &quot;Talha ibn Ubaydullah called me over and we made a mutual agreement that he would make an exchange for me. He took the gold and turned it about in his hand, and then said, &#x27;I can&#x27;t do it until my treasurer brings the money to me from al-Ghaba.&#x27; Umar ibn al- Khattab was listening and Umar said, &#x27;By Allah! Do not leave him until you have taken it from him!&#x27; Then he said, &#x27;The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;Gold for silver is usury except hand to hand. Wheat for wheat is usury except hand to hand. Dates for dates is usury except hand to hand. Barley for barley is usury except hand to hand.&quot; &quot;&#x27; Malik said, &quot;When a man buys dirhams with dinars and then finds a bad dirham among them and wants to return it, the exchange of the dinars breaks down, and he returns the silver and takes back his dinars. The explanation of what is disapproved of in that is that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &#x27;Gold for silver is usury except hand to hand.&#x27; and Umar ibn al-Khattab said, &#x27;If someone asks you to wait to be paid until he has gone back to his house, do not leave him.&#x27; When he returns a dirham to him from the exchange after he has left him, it is like a debt or something deferred. For that reason, it is disapproved of, and the exchange collapses. Umar ibn al-Khattab wanted that all gold, silver and food should not be sold for goods to be paid later. He did not want there to be any delay or deferment in any such sale, whether it involved one commodity or different sorts of commodities</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1327]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1327" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">मालिक बिन औस (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा: मुझे हाजत हुई सौ दीनार के दिरहम लेने की, तो मुझे बुलाया तलहा बिन उबैदुल्लाह (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने। फिर हम दोनों राजी हुए सर्फ़ के ऊपर और उन्होंने दीनार मुझ से ले लिए और हाथ से उलट-पलट करने लगे और कहा: सब्र करो यहां तक के मेरा ख़ज़ांची ग़ाबा आ जाए। हज़रत उमर (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने ये सुन कर कहा: नहीं, क़सम अल्लाह की, मत छोड़ना तलहा को ब ग़ैर रुपये लिए। फ़रमाया रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने: सोने का बेचना चांदी के बदले में रिबा है मगर जब नक़दन-नक़द हो, और गेहूं बदले गेहूं के बेचना रिबा है मगर नक़दन-नक़द, और खजूर बदले खजूर के बेचना रिबा है मगर नक़दन-नक़द, और जौ बदले जौ के बेचना रिबा है मगर नक़दन-नक़द, और नमक बदले नमक के बेचना रिबा है मगर नक़दन-नक़द। कहा मालिक ने: अगर किसी शख़्स ने रुपये अशरफ़ियों के बदले में लिए फिर उस में एक रुपया खोटा निकला, अब उसको फेरना चाहे तो सब अशरफ़ियां अपनी फेर ले और सब रुपये उसके वापस दे दे, क्योंकि आंहज़रत (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: सोना बदले में चांदी के रिबा है मगर जब नक़दन-नक़द हो। और हज़रत उमर ने फ़रमाया: अगर तुझ से अपने घर जाने की मोहलत मांगे तो मोहलत न दे। अगर एक रुपया उसको फेर देगा और उस से जुदा हो जाएगा तो मिस्ल-ए-दैन या मीआद के हो जाएगा, इसी वास्ते ये मकरूह है। ख़ुद इस बेच को तोड़ डालना चाहिए के एक तरफ़ नक़द हो, दूसरे तरफ़ वादा, ख़्वाह एक जिन्स हो या कई जिन्स हों।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1327]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1327" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">مالک بن اوس نے کہا مجھے حاجت ہوئی سو دینار کے درہم لینے کی تو مجھے بلایا طلحہ بن عبیداللہ نے پھر ہم دونوں راضی ہوئے صرف کے اوپر اور انہوں نے دینار مجھ سے لے لئے اور ہاتھ سے لٹ پلٹ کرنے لگے اور کہا صبر کرو یہاں تک کہ میرا خزانچی غابہ آ جائے حضرت عمر نے یہ سن کر کہا نہیں قسم اللہ کی مت چھوڑنا طلحہ کو بغیر روپے لئے فرمایا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے سونے کا بیچنا چاندی کے بدلے میں ربا ہے مگر جب نقدا نقد ہو اور گہیوں بدلے گیہوں کے بیچنا ربا ہے مگر نقدا نقد اور کھجور بدلے کھجور کے بیچنا رہا ہے مگر نقدا نقد اور جو بدلے جو کے بیچنا رہا ہے مگر نقدا نقد اور نمک بدلے نمک کے بیچنا رہا ہے مگر نقدا نقد ۔ کہا مالک نے اگر کسی شخص نے روپے اشرفیوں کے بدلے میں لئے پھر اس میں ایک روپیہ کھوٹا نکلا اب اس کو پھیرنا چاہے تو سب اشرفیاں اپنی پھیر لے اور سب روپے اس کے واپس دے دے کیونکہ آنحضرت صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا سونا بدلے میں چاندی کے ربا ہے مگر جب نقدا نقد ہو اور حضرت عمر نے فرمایا اگر تجھ سے اپنے گھر جانے کی مہلت مانگے تو مہلت نہ دے اگر ایک روپیہ اس کو پھیر دے گا اور اس سے جدا ہو جائے گا تو مثل دین کے یا میعاد کے ہو جائے گا اسی واسطے یہ مکروہ ہے خود اس بیع کو توڑ ڈالنا چاہیے کہ ایک طرف نقد ہو دوسرے طرف وعدہ خواہ ایک جنس یا کئی جنس ہوں ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1327]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1327" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Malik bin Aus (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha: Mujhe haajat hui sau dinar ke dirham lene ki, to mujhe bulaya Talha bin Ubaidullah (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne. Phir hum dono raazi hue sarf ke upar aur unhone dinar mujh se le liye aur haath se ulat-palat karne lage aur kaha: Sabr karo yahan tak ke mera khazanchi ghaba aa jaye. Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne ye sun kar kaha: Nahi, qasam Allah ki, mat chhodna Talha ko baghair rupaye liye. Farmaya Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne: Sone ka bechna chandi ke badle mein riba hai magar jab naqdan-naqd ho, aur gehoon badle gehoon ke bechna riba hai magar naqdan-naqd, aur khajoor badle khajoor ke bechna riba hai magar naqdan-naqd, aur jau badle jau ke bechna riba hai magar naqdan-naqd, aur namak badle namak ke bechna riba hai magar naqdan-naqd. Kaha Malik ne: Agar kisi shakhs ne rupaye ashrafiyon ke badle mein liye phir us mein ek rupiya khota nikla, ab usko pherna chahe to sab ashrafiyan apni pher le aur sab rupaye uske wapas de de, kyunke Aanhat (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Sona badle mein chandi ke riba hai magar jab naqdan-naqd ho. Aur Hazrat Umar ne farmaya: Agar tujh se apne ghar jaane ki mohlat mange to mohlat na de. Agar ek rupiya usko pher dega aur us se juda ho jayega to misl-e-dain ya mi&#x27;aad ke ho jayega, isi waste ye makrooh hai. Khud is bech ko tod dalna chahiye ke ek taraf naqd ho, dusre taraf wada, khwah ek jins ho ya kai jins hon.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1327]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1327" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 1314

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 31</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1328]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1328" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1328</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1328 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَزِيدَ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ قُسَيْطٍ، أَنَّهُ رَأَى سَعِيدَ بْنَ الْمُسَيَّبِ يُرَاطِلُ الذَّهَبَ بِالذَّهَبِ فَيُفْرِغُ ذَهَبَهُ فِي كِفَّةِ الْمِيزَانِ وَيُفْرِغُ صَاحِبُهُ الَّذِي يُرَاطِلُهُ ذَهَبَهُ فِي كِفَّةِ الْمِيزَانِ الأُخْرَى فَإِذَا اعْتَدَلَ لِسَانُ الْمِيزَانِ أَخَذَ وَأَعْطَى ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ الأَمْرُ عِنْدَنَا فِي بَيْعِ الذَّهَبِ بِالذَّهَبِ وَالْوَرِقِ بِالْوَرِقِ مُرَاطَلَةً أَنَّهُ لاَ بَأْسَ بِذَلِكَ أَنْ يَأْخُذَ أَحَدَ عَشَرَ دِينَارًا بِعَشَرَةِ دَنَانِيرَ يَدًا بِيَدٍ إِذَا كَانَ وَزْنُ الذَّهَبَيْنِ سَوَاءً عَيْنًا بِعَيْنٍ وَإِنْ تَفَاضَلَ الْعَدَدُ وَالدَّرَاهِمُ أَيْضًا فِي ذَلِكَ بِمَنْزِلَةِ الدَّنَانِيرِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ مَنْ رَاطَلَ ذَهَبًا بِذَهَبٍ أَوْ وَرِقًا بِوَرِقٍ فَكَانَ بَيْنَ الذَّهَبَيْنِ فَضْلُ مِثْقَالٍ فَأَعْطَى صَاحِبَهُ قِيمَتَهُ مِنَ الْوَرِقِ أَوْ مِنْ غَيْرِهَا فَلاَ يَأْخُذُهُ فَإِنَّ ذَلِكَ قَبِيحٌ وَذَرِيعَةٌ إِلَى الرِّبَا لأَنَّهُ إِذَا جَازَ لَهُ أَنْ يَأْخُذَ الْمِثْقَالَ بِقِيمَتِهِ حَتَّى كَأَنَّهُ اشْتَرَاهُ عَلَى حِدَتِهِ جَازَ لَهُ أَنْ يَأْخُذَ الْمِثْقَالَ بِقِيمَتِهِ مِرَارًا لأَنْ يُجِيزَ ذَلِكَ الْبَيْعَ بَيْنَهُ وَبَيْنَ صَاحِبِهِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَلَوْ أَنَّهُ بَاعَهُ ذَلِكَ الْمِثْقَالَ مُفْرَدًا لَيْسَ مَعَهُ غَيْرُهُ لَمْ يَأْخُذْهُ بِعُشْرِ الثَّمَنِ الَّذِي أَخَذَهُ بِهِ لأَنْ يُجَوِّزَ لَهُ الْبَيْعَ فَذَلِكَ الذَّرِيعَةُ إِلَى إِحْلاَلِ الْحَرَامِ وَالأَمْرُ الْمَنْهِيُّ عَنْهُ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فِي الرَّجُلِ يُرَاطِلُ الرَّجُلَ وَيُعْطِيهِ الذَّهَبَ الْعُتُقَ الْجِيَادَ وَيَجْعَلُ مَعَهَا تِبْرًا ذَهَبًا غَيْرَ جَيِّدَةٍ وَيَأْخُذُ مِنْ صَاحِبِهِ ذَهَبًا كُوفِيَّةً مُقَطَّعَةً وَتِلْكَ الْكُوفِيَّةُ مَكْرُوهَةٌ عِنْدَ النَّاسِ فَيَتَبَايَعَانِ ذَلِكَ مِثْلاً بِمِثْلٍ إِنَّ ذَلِكَ لاَ يَصْلُحُ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَتَفْسِيرُ مَا كُرِهَ مِنْ ذَلِكَ أَنَّ صَاحِبَ الذَّهَبِ الْجِيَادِ أَخَذَ فَضْلَ عُيُونِ ذَهَبِهِ فِي التِّبْرِ الَّذِي طَرَحَ مَعَ ذَهَبِهِ وَلَوْلاَ فَضْلُ ذَهَبِهِ عَلَى ذَهَبِ صَاحِبِهِ لَمْ يُرَاطِلْهُ صَاحِبُهُ بِتِبْرِهِ ذَلِكَ إِلَى ذَهَبِهِ الْكُوفِيَّةِ فَامْتَنَعَ وَإِنَّمَا مَثَلُ ذَلِكَ كَمَثَلِ رَجُلٍ أَرَادَ أَنْ يَبْتَاعَ ثَلاَثَةَ أَصْوُعٍ مِنْ تَمْرٍ عَجْوَةٍ بِصَاعَيْنِ وَمُدٍّ مِنْ تَمْرٍ كَبِيسٍ فَقِيلَ لَهُ هَذَا لاَ يَصْلُحُ ‏.‏ فَجَعَلَ صَاعَيْنِ مِنْ كَبِيسٍ وَصَاعًا مِنْ حَشَفٍ يُرِيدُ أَنْ يُجِيزَ بِذَلِكَ بَيْعَهُ فَذَلِكَ لاَ يَصْلُحُ لأَنَّهُ لَمْ يَكُنْ صَاحِبُ الْعَجْوَةِ لِيُعْطِيَهُ صَاعًا مِنَ الْعَجْوَةِ بِصَاعٍ مِنْ حَشَفٍ وَلَكِنَّهُ إِنَّمَا أَعْطَاهُ ذَلِكَ لِفَضْلِ الْكَبِيسِ أَوْ أَنْ يَقُولَ الرَّجُلُ لِلرَّجُلِ بِعْنِي ثَلاَثَةَ أَصْوُعٍ مِنَ الْبَيْضَاءِ بِصَاعَيْنِ وَنِصْفٍ مِنْ حِنْطَةٍ شَامِيَّةٍ فَيَقُولُ هَذَا لاَ يَصْلُحُ إِلاَّ مِثْلاً بِمِثْلٍ ‏.‏ فَيَجْعَلُ صَاعَيْنِ مِنْ حِنْطَةٍ شَامِيَّةٍ وَصَاعًا مِنْ شَعِيرٍ يُرِيدُ أَنْ يُجِيزَ بِذَلِكَ الْبَيْعَ فِيمَا بَيْنَهُمَا فَهَذَا لاَ يَصْلُحُ لأَنَّهُ لَمْ يَكُنْ لِيُعْطِيَهُ بِصَاعٍ مِنْ شَعِيرٍ صَاعًا مِنْ حِنْطَةٍ بَيْضَاءَ لَوْ كَانَ ذَلِكَ الصَّاعُ مُفْرَدًا وَإِنَّمَا أَعْطَاهُ إِيَّاهُ لِفَضْلِ الشَّامِيَّةِ عَلَى الْبَيْضَاءِ فَهَذَا لاَ يَصْلُحُ وَهُوَ مِثْلُ مَا وَصَفْنَا مِنَ التِّبْرِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فَكُلُّ شَىْءٍ مِنَ الذَّهَبِ وَالْوَرِقِ وَالطَّعَامِ كُلِّهِ الَّذِي لاَ يَنْبَغِي أَنْ يُبَاعَ إِلاَّ مِثْلاً بِمِثْلٍ فَلاَ يَنْبَغِي أَنْ يُجْعَلَ مَعَ الصِّنْفِ الْجَيِّدِ مِنَ الْمَرْغُوبِ فِيهِ الشَّىْءُ الرَّدِيءُ الْمَسْخُوطُ لِيُجَازَ الْبَيْعُ وَلِيُسْتَحَلَّ بِذَلِكَ مَا نُهِيَ عَنْهُ مِنَ الأَمْرِ الَّذِي لاَ يَصْلُحُ إِذَا جُعِلَ ذَلِكَ مَعَ الصِّنْفِ الْمَرْغُوبِ فِيهِ وَإِنَّمَا يُرِيدُ صَاحِبُ ذَلِكَ أَنْ يُدْرِكَ بِذَلِكَ فَضْلَ جَوْدَةِ مَا يَبِيعُ فَيُعْطِيَ الشَّىْءَ الَّذِي لَوْ أَعْطَاهُ وَحْدَهُ لَمْ يَقْبَلْهُ صَاحِبُهُ وَلَمْ يَهْمُمْ بِذَلِكَ وَإِنَّمَا يَقْبَلُهُ مِنْ أَجْلِ الَّذِي يَأْخُذُ مَعَهُ لِفَضْلِ سِلْعَةِ صَاحِبِهِ عَلَى سِلْعَتِهِ فَلاَ يَنْبَغِي لِشَىْءٍ مِنَ الذَّهَبِ وَالْوَرِقِ وَالطَّعَامِ أَنْ يَدْخُلَهُ شَىْءٌ مِنْ هَذِهِ الصِّفَةِ فَإِنْ أَرَادَ صَاحِبُ الطَّعَامِ الرَّدِيءِ أَنْ يَبِيعَهُ بِغَيْرِهِ فَلْيَبِعْهُ عَلَى حِدَتِهِ وَلاَ يَجْعَلْ مَعَ ذَلِكَ شَيْئًا فَلاَ بَأْسَ بِهِ إِذَا كَانَ كَذَلِكَ ‏.‏</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: Said ibn al-Musayyab (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that Yazid ibn Abdullah ibn Qusayt saw Said ibn al-Musayyab sell gold counterpoising for gold. He poured his gold into one pan of the scales, and the man with whom he was counterpoising put his gold in the other pan of the scale and when the tongue of the scales was balanced, they took and gave.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1328]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1328" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1328</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1328 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said, &quot;According to the way things are done among us there is no harm in selling gold for gold, and silver for silver by counterpoising weight, even if 11 dinars are taken for 10 dinars hand to hand, when the weight of gold is equal, coin for coin, even if the number is different. Dirhams in such a situation are treated the same way as dinars.&quot; Malik said, &quot;If, when counterpoising gold for gold or silver for silver, there is a difference of weight, one party should not give the other the value of the difference in silver or something else. Such a transaction is ugly and a means to usury because if one of the parties were permitted to take the difference for a separate price, it could be as if he had bought it separately, so he would be permitted. Then it would be possible for him to ask for many times the value of the difference in order to permit the completion of the transaction between the two parties. Malik said, &quot;If he had really been sold the difference without anything else with it, he would not have taken it for a tenth of the price for which he took it in order to put a &#x27;legal front&#x27; on the transaction. This leads to allowing what is forbidden . The matter is forbidden.&quot; Malik said that it was not good when counterpoising to give good old gold coins and put along with them unminted gold in exchange for worn kufic gold, which was unpopular and to then treat the exchange as like for like. Malik said, &quot;The commentary on why that is disapproved is that the owner of the good gold uses the excellence of his old gold coins as an excuse to throw in the unminted gold with it. Had it not been for the superiority of his (good) gold over the gold of the other party, the other party would not have counterpoised the unminted gold for his kufic gold, and the deal would have been refused. &quot;It is like a man wanting to buy three sa of ajwa dried dates for two sa and a mudd of kabis dates, and on being told that it was not good, then offering two sa of kabis and a sa of poor dates desiring to make the sale possible. That is not good because the owner of the ajwa should not give him a sa of ajwa for a sa of poor dates. He would only give him that because of the excellence of kabis dates. &quot;Or it is like a man asking some one to sell him three sa of white wheat for two and a half sa of Syrian wheat, and being told that it was not good except like for like, and so offering two sa of wheat and one sa of barley intending to make the sale possible between them. That is not good because no one would have given a sa of barley for a sa of white wheat had that sa been by itself. It was only given because of the excellence of Syrian wheat over the white wheat. This is not good. It is the same as the case of the unminted gold.&quot; Malik said, &quot;Where gold, silver and food, things which should only be sold like for like, are concerned, something disliked and of poor quality should not be put with something good and desirable in order to make the sale possible and to make a bad situation halal. When something of desirable quality is put with something of poor quality and it is only included so that its excellence in quality is noticed, something is being sold which if it had been sold on its own, would not have been accepted and to which the buyer would not have paid any attention. It is only accepted by the buyer because of the superiority of what comes with it over his own goods. Transactions involving gold, silver, or food, must not have anything of this description enter into them. If the owner of the poor quality goods wants to sell them, he sells them on their own, and does not put anything with them. There is no harm if it is like that</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1328" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">यज़ीद बिन अब्दुल्लाह बिन क़सित ने कहा: सईद बिन मुसैय्यब (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) को देखा जब सोने को सोने के बदले में बेचते तो अपने सोने को एक पल्ला में रखते और दूसरा शख़्स अपने सोने को दूसरे पल्ले में रखता, जब तराज़ू का कांटा बराबर हो जाता तो दूसरे का सोना ले लेता और अपना सोना दे देता। कहा मालिक ने: जो शख़्स सोने को सोने के बदले में तौल कर बेचे तो कुछ क़बाहत नहीं, अगरचे एक पलड़े में ग्यारह दीनार चढ़ें और दूसरी तरफ़ दस दीनार, जब नक़दन-नक़द हों और वज़न बराबर हो, अगरचे शुमार में कम-ज़्यादा हों, ऐसा ही दिरहम का हुक्म है।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1328" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>
