<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">یزید بن عبداللہ بن قسیط نے کہا سعید بن مسیب کو دیکھا جب سونے کو سونے کے بدلے میں بیچتے تو اپنے سونے کو ایک پلہ میں رکھتے اور دوسرا شخص اپنے سونے کو دوسرے پلے میں رکھتا جب ترازو کا کاٹنا برابر ہو جاتا تو دوسرے کا سونا لے لیتا اور اپنا سونا دے دیتے۔ کہا مالک نے جو شخص سونے کو سونے کے بدلے میں تول کر بیچے تو کچھ قباحت نہیں اگرچہ ایک پلڑے میں گیارہ دینار چڑھیں اور دوسری طرف دس دینار جب نقدا نقد ہوں اور وزن برابر ہو اگرچہ شمار میں کم زیادہ ہوں ایسا ہی دراہم کا حکم ہے۔</p>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1328]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1328" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Roman Urdu · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1328</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1328 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Yazid bin Abdullah bin Qasit ne kaha: Saeed bin Musayyib (Radi Allahu Anhu) ko dekha jab sone ko sone ke badle mein bechte to apne sone ko ek palla mein rakhte aur dusra shakhs apne sone ko dusre palle mein rakhta, jab tarazu ka kanta barabar ho jata to dusre ka sona le leta aur apna sona de deta. Kaha Malik ne: Jo shakhs sone ko sone ke badle mein taul kar beche to kuch qabahat nahi, agarche ek palre mein gyarah dinar chadhen aur dusri taraf das dinar, jab naqdan-naqd hon aur wazan barabar ho, agarche shumar mein kam-zyada hon, aisa hi dirham ka hukm hai.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1328" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1315

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1329)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 31</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ مَنِ ابْتَاعَ طَعَامًا فَلاَ يَبِعْهُ حَتَّى يَسْتَوْفِيَهُ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1329]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1329" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Nafi from Abdullah ibn Umar that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;Someone who buys food, must not resell it until he takes delivery of it all</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1329]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1329" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">यहया ने मुझे मालिक से, उन्होंने नाफ़े से, उन्होंने अब्दुल्लाह इब्न उमर से बयान किया के रसूल अल्लाह सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम ने फ़रमाया: &#x27;जो कोई खाना (अनाज) खरीदे, तो उसे उस वक़्त तक दोबारा न बेचे जब तक के वो उस पर मुकम्मल क़ब्ज़ा न कर ले।&#x27;</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1329]</p>

## Narration 1316

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ دِينَارٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ مَنِ ابْتَاعَ طَعَامًا فَلاَ يَبِعْهُ حَتَّى يَقْبِضَهُ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1330]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1330" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

## Narration 1317

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1331)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 31</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ كُنَّا فِي زَمَانِ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم نَبْتَاعُ الطَّعَامَ فَيَبْعَثُ عَلَيْنَا مَنْ يَأْمُرُنَا بِانْتِقَالِهِ مِنَ الْمَكَانِ الَّذِي ابْتَعْنَاهُ فِيهِ إِلَى مَكَانٍ سِوَاهُ قَبْلَ أَنْ نَبِيعَهُ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1331]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1331" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1331</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1331 ]</p>

</details>

<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Nafi that Abdullah ibn Umar said, &quot;In the time of the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, we used to buy food. He sent orders for us to move our purchases from the place in which we purchased them to another place before we re-sold them</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1331]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1331" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1331</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1331 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुल्लाह बिन उमर (रदि अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा हम रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के ज़माने में अनाज खरीदते थे, फिर आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) हमारे पास एक आदमी भेजते थे, वोह हम को हुकुम करता था के गल्ला उस जगह से उठा ले जाएं जहां खरीदा है, क़ब्ल इसके के हम उसको बेच दें।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1331]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1331" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">عبداللہ بن عمر نے کہا ہم رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے زمانے میں اناج خریدتے تھے پھر آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم ہمارے پاس ایک آدمی بھیجتے تھے وہ ہم کو حکم کرتا تھا کہ غلہ اس جگہ سے اٹھا لے جائیں جہاں خریدا ہے قبل اس کے کہ ہم اس کو بیع کریں ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1331]</p>

## Narration 1318

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1332)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 31</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1332]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1332" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1332</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1332 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، ‏.‏ أَنَّ حَكِيمَ بْنَ حِزَامٍ، ابْتَاعَ طَعَامًا أَمَرَ بِهِ عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ لِلنَّاسِ فَبَاعَ حَكِيمٌ الطَّعَامَ قَبْلَ أَنْ يَسْتَوْفِيَهُ فَبَلَغَ ذَلِكَ عُمَرَ بْنَ الْخَطَّابِ فَرَدَّهُ عَلَيْهِ وَقَالَ لاَ تَبِعْ طَعَامًا ابْتَعْتَهُ حَتَّى تَسْتَوْفِيَهُ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1332" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Hakim ibn Hizam (companion); Umar ibn al-Khattab (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Nafi that Hakim ibn Hizam traded in food for people as Umar ibn al-Khattab had ordered him to do. Hakim re-sold the food before he had taken delivery of it. That reached Umar ibn al-Khattab and he revoked the sale and said, &quot;Do not sell food which you have purchased until you take delivery of it</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1332]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1332" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">यह्या ने मुझे मालिक से, उन्होंने नाफ़ि से बयान किया के हकीम इब्न हिज़ाम लोगों के लिए अनाज की तिजारत करते थे जैसा के उमर इब्न अल-खत्ताब ने उन्हें हुक्म दिया था। हकीम ने अनाज उस वक़्त बेचा जब अभी उस पर क़ब्ज़ा नहीं किया था। जब यह बात उमर इब्न अल-खत्ताब तक पहुंची तो उन्होंने इस सौदे को मंसूख कर दिया और कहा: &#x27;खरीदे हुए अनाज को उस वक़्त तक न बेचो जब तक के तुम उस पर क़ब्ज़ा न कर लो।&#x27;</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1332" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">یحییٰ نے مجھے مالک سے، انہوں نے نافع سے بیان کیا کہ حکیم بن حزام لوگوں کے لیے اناج کی تجارت کرتے تھے جیسا کہ عمر بن الخطاب نے انہیں حکم دیا تھا۔ حکیم نے اناج اس وقت بیچا جب ابھی اس پر قبضہ نہیں کیا تھا۔ جب یہ بات عمر بن الخطاب تک پہنچی تو انہوں نے اس سودے کو منسوخ کر دیا اور کہا: &#x27;خریدے ہوئے اناج کو اس وقت تک نہ بیچو جب تک کہ تم اس پر قبضہ نہ کر لو۔&#x27;</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1332" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya ne mujhe Malik se, unhone Nafi se bayan kiya ke Hakim ibn Hizam logon ke liye anaj ki tijarat karte thay jaisa ke Umar ibn al-Khattab ne unhe hukm diya tha. Hakim ne anaj us waqt becha jab abhi us par qabza nahi kiya tha. Jab yeh baat Umar ibn al-Khattab tak pahunchi to unhone is saude ko mansookh kar diya aur kaha: &#x27;Kharide hue anaj ko us waqt tak na becho jab tak ke tum us par qabza na kar lo.&#x27;</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1332" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1319

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1333)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 31</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1333]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1333" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1333</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1333 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ ‏.‏ أَنَّ صُكُوكًا، خَرَجَتْ لِلنَّاسِ فِي زَمَانِ مَرْوَانَ بْنِ الْحَكَمِ مِنْ طَعَامِ الْجَارِ فَتَبَايَعَ النَّاسُ تِلْكَ الصُّكُوكَ بَيْنَهُمْ قَبْلَ أَنْ يَسْتَوْفُوهَا فَدَخَلَ زَيْدُ بْنُ ثَابِتٍ وَرَجُلٌ مِنْ أَصْحَابِ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم عَلَى مَرْوَانَ بْنِ الْحَكَمِ فَقَالاَ أَتُحِلُّ بَيْعَ الرِّبَا يَا مَرْوَانُ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ أَعُوذُ بِاللَّهِ وَمَا ذَاكَ فَقَالاَ هَذِهِ الصُّكُوكُ تَبَايَعَهَا النَّاسُ ثُمَّ بَاعُوهَا قَبْلَ أَنْ يَسْتَوْفُوهَا فَبَعَثَ مَرْوَانُ الْحَرَسَ يَتْبَعُونَهَا يَنْزِعُونَهَا مِنْ أَيْدِي النَّاسِ وَيَرُدُّونَهَا إِلَى أَهْلِهَا ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1333" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: historical report. Attribution: Zayd Thabit (unresolved); Marwan ibn al-Hakam (unresolved); unnamed in the supplied field (unresolved). The source historical episode names Zayd Thabit with that exact spelling and Marwan, and mentions one unnamed Companion. No missing name component or identity is supplied.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he had heard that receipts were given to people in the time of Marwan ibn al-Hakam for the produce of the market at al-Jar. People bought and sold the receipts among themselves before they took delivery of the goods. Zayd Thabit and one of the Companions of the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, went to Marwan ibn al-Hakam and said, &quot;Marwan! Do you make usury halal?&quot; He said, &quot;I seek refuge with Allah! What is that?&quot; He said, &quot;These receipts which people buy and sell before they take delivery of the goods.&quot; Marwan therefore sent a guard to follow them and to take them from people&#x27;s hands and return them to their owners</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1333]</p>

<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1333]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1333" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">नफ़ी से रिवायत है के हकीम बिन हिज़ाम ने ग़ल्ला खरीदा जो हज़रात उम्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने लोगों को दिलवाया था. फिर हकीम बिन हिज़ाम ने उस ग़ल्ले को बेच डाला क़ब्ज़े से पहले. जब हज़रात उम्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) को इसकी खबर पहुंची बीच जब तक उस पर कब्जा न कर ले. इमाम मालिक को पहुंचा के मरवान बिन हकम के अहद-इ-हुकूमत में लोगों को सनदें (रिसीट्स) मिलीं &#x27;जार&#x27; के गल्ले की में लाएं. तोह ज़ैद बिन थाबित और एक और सहबी मरवान के पास गए और कहा क्या तू रिबा को दुरूस्त जानता है ए मरवान? मरवान ने कहा माँ&#x27;अज़ल्लाह क्या कहते हो? उन्हों ने कहा के ये सनदें जिन लोगों ने खरीदा फिर खरीद कर दोबारा बेचा क़ब्ल-इ-ग़ल्ला लेने के. मरवान ने चौकीदार को भेजा कि वह सनदें लोगों से छीन कर संद वालों के हवाले कर दें. इमाम मालिक को पहुंचे एक शख्स ने अनाज खरीदना चाहा एक शख्स से वादे पर तोह बाए (बेचने वाला) मुश्तरी (खरीदने वाला) को बाजार में ले गया और उसको बोर दिखा कर कहने लगा कौन से ग़ल्ले में तुम्हारे वास्ते खरीद करूँ? मुश्तरी ने कहा क्या तू मेरे हाथ उस चीज़ का बेचता है जो खुद तेरे पास नहीं है? फिर बाए और मुश्तरी दोनों अब्दुल्लाह बिन उम्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) के पास आये और उनसे बयां किया. अब्दुल्लाह बिन उम्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने मुश्तरी से कहा मत खरीदो उस चीज़ को जो बाये के पास नहीं है और बाए से कहा मत बेच उस चीज़ को जो तेरे पास नहीं है.</p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">نافع سے روایت ہے کہ حکیم بن حزام نے غلہ خریدا جو حضرت عمر نے لوگوں کو دلوایا تھا پھر حکیم بن حزام نے اس غلہ کو بیچ ڈالا قبضہ سے پہلے جب حضرت عمر کو اس کی خبر پہنچی آپ نے وہ غلہ حکیم بن حزام کو پھر وا دیا اور کہا جس غلہ کو تو خریدے پھر اس کو مت بیچ جب تک اس پر قبضہ نہ کر لے ۔ امام مالک کو پہنچا کہ مروان بن حکم کے عہد حکومت میں لوگوں کو سندیں ملیں جار کے غلہ کی لوگوں نے ان سندوں کو بیچا ایک دوسرے کے ہاتھ قبل اس بات کے کہ غلہ اپنے قبضة میں لائیں تو زید بن ثابت اور ایک اور صحابہ مروان کے پاس گئے اور کہا کیا تو ربا کو درست جانتا ہے اے مروان مروان نے کہا معاذاللہ کیا کہتے ہو انہوں نے کہا کہ یہ سندیں جن لوگوں نے خریدا پھر خرید کر دوبارہ بیچا قبلہ غلہ لینے کے مروان نے چوکیدار کو بھیجا کہ وہ سندیں لوگوں سے چھین کر سند والوں کے حوالے کر دیں ۔ امام مالک کو پہنچا ایک شخص نے اناج خریدنا چاہا ایک شخص سے وعدے پر تو بائع (بچنے والا) مشتری (خریدنے والا) کو بازار میں لے گیا اور اس کو بورے دکھا کر کہنے لگا کون سے غلہ میں تمہاری واسطے خرید کروں مشتری (خریدنے والا) نے کہا کیا تو میرے ہاتھ اس چیز کا بیچتا ہے جو خود تیرے پاس نہیں ہے پھر بائع (بچنے والا) اور مشتری (خریدنے والا اور بیچنے والا) دونوں عبداللہ بن عمر کے پاس آئے اور ان سے بیان کیا عبداللہ بن عمر نے مشتری (خریدنے والا) سے کہا مت خریدو اس چیز کو جو بائع (بچنے والا) کے پاس نہیں ہے اور بائع (بچنے والا) سے کہا مت بیچ اس چیز کو جو تیرے پاس نہیں ہے ۔</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1333 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Nafi se riwayat hai ke Hakeem bin Hizam ne ghalla khareeda jo Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne logon ko dilwaya tha. Phir Hakeem bin Hizam ne us ghalle ko bech dala qabze se pehle. Jab Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ko iski khabar pahunchi, Aap ne woh ghalla Hakeem bin Hizam ko phir wapis dilwa diya aur kaha jis ghalle ko tu khareede phir usko mat bech jab tak us par qabza na kar le. Imam Malik ko pahuncha ke Marwan bin Hakam ke ahd-e-hukumat mein logon ko sandein (receipts) milin &#x27;Jaar&#x27; ke ghalle ki, logon ne un sandon ko becha ek dusre ke hath qabl is baat ke ke ghalla apne qabze mein layein. Toh Zaid bin Thabit aur ek aur sahabi Marwan ke paas gaye aur kaha kya tu riba ko durust janta hai aye Marwan? Marwan ne kaha Ma&#x27;azAllah kya kehte ho? Unhon ne kaha ke ye sandein jin logon ne khareeda phir khareed kar dobara becha qabl-e-ghalla lene ke. Marwan ne chowkidaar ko bheja ke woh sandein logon se cheen kar sand walon ke hawale kar dein. Imam Malik ko pahuncha ek shakhs ne anaaj khareedna chaha ek shakhs se wade par toh baaye (bechne wala) mushtari (khareedne wala) ko bazaar mein le gaya aur usko bore dikha kar kehne laga kaun se ghalle mein tumhare waste khareed karun? Mushtari ne kaha kya tu mere hath us cheez ka bechta hai jo khud tere paas nahi hai? Phir baaye aur mushtari dono Abdullah bin Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ke paas aaye aur unse bayan kiya. Abdullah bin Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne mushtari se kaha mat khareedo us cheez ko jo baaye ke paas nahi hai aur baaye se kaha mat bech us cheez ko jo tere paas nahi hai.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1333" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1320

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 31</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1334]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1334" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1334</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1334 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ رَجُلاً، أَرَادَ أَنْ يَبْتَاعَ طَعَامًا مِنْ رَجُلٍ إِلَى أَجَلٍ فَذَهَبَ بِهِ الرَّجُلُ الَّذِي يُرِيدُ أَنْ يَبِيعَهُ الطَّعَامَ إِلَى السُّوقِ فَجَعَلَ يُرِيهِ الصُّبَرَ وَيَقُولُ لَهُ مِنْ أَيِّهَا تُحِبُّ أَنْ أَبْتَاعَ لَكَ فَقَالَ الْمُبْتَاعُ أَتَبِيعُنِي مَا لَيْسَ عِنْدَكَ فَأَتَيَا عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ عُمَرَ فَذَكَرَا ذَلِكَ لَهُ فَقَالَ عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ عُمَرَ لِلْمُبْتَاعِ لاَ تَبْتَعْ مِنْهُ مَا لَيْسَ عِنْدَهُ ‏.‏ وَقَالَ لِلْبَائِعِ لاَ تَبِعْ مَا لَيْسَ عِنْدَكَ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1334" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abdallah ibn Umar (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he had heard that a man wanted to buy food from a man in advance. The man who wanted to sell the food to him went with him to the market, and he began to show him heaps, saying, &quot;Which one would you like me to buy for you.&quot; The buyer said to him, &quot;Are you selling me what you do not have?&quot; So they came to Abdullah ibn Umarand mentioned that to him. Abdallah ibn Umar said to the buyer, &quot;Do not buy from him what he does not have.&quot; He said to the seller, &quot;Do not sell what you do not have</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1334]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1334" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1334 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">यह्या ने मुझे मालिक से बयान किया के एक शख़्स ने एक आदमी से पेशगी अनाज खरीदना चाहा। बेचने वाला उसे बाज़ार ले गया और ढेर दिखाने लगा, पूछने लगा: &#x27;कौन सा खरीदूं?&#x27; खरीदार ने कहा: &#x27;क्या तुम मुझे वोह चीज़ बेच रहे हो जो तुम्हारे पास है ही नहीं?&#x27; फिर वोह अब्दुल्लाह इब्न उमर के पास गए, तो अब्दुल्लाह इब्न उमर ने खरीदार से कहा: &#x27;जो इस के पास नहीं वोह मत खरीदो।&#x27; और बेचने वाले से कहा: &#x27;जो तुम्हारे पास नहीं है उसे मत बेचो।&#x27;</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1334" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<h3>Urdu</h3>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">یحییٰ نے مجھے مالک سے بیان کیا کہ ایک شخص نے ایک آدمی سے پیشگی اناج خریدنا چاہا۔ بیچنے والا اسے بازار لے گیا اور ڈھیر دکھانے لگا، پوچھنے لگا: &#x27;کون سا خریدوں؟&#x27; خریدار نے کہا: &#x27;کیا تم مجھے وہ چیز بیچ رہے ہو جو تمہارے پاس ہے ہی نہیں؟&#x27; پھر وہ عبداللہ بن عمر کے پاس گئے، تو عبداللہ بن عمر نے خریدار سے کہا: &#x27;جو اس کے پاس نہیں وہ مت خریدو۔&#x27; اور بیچنے والے سے کہا: &#x27;جو تمہارے پاس نہیں ہے اسے مت بیچو۔&#x27;</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1334" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya ne mujhe Malik se bayan kiya ke ek shakhs ne ek aadmi se peshgi anaj kharidna chaha. Bechne wala use bazaar le gaya aur dher dikhane laga, puchne laga: &#x27;Kaun sa kharidu?&#x27; Kharidar ne kaha: &#x27;Kya tum mujhe woh cheez bech rahe ho jo tumhare paas hai hi nahi?&#x27; Phir woh Abdullah ibn Umar ke paas gaye, to Abdullah ibn Umar ne kharidar se kaha: &#x27;Jo is ke paas nahi woh mat kharido.&#x27; Aur bechne wale se kaha: &#x27;Jo tumhare paas nahi hai use mat becho.&#x27;</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1334" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1321

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1335)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 31</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1335]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1335" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1335</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1335 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، أَنَّهُ سَمِعَ جَمِيلَ بْنَ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ الْمُؤَذِّنَ، يَقُولُ لِسَعِيدِ بْنِ الْمُسَيَّبِ إِنِّي رَجُلٌ أَبْتَاعُ مِنَ الأَرْزَاقِ الَّتِي تُعْطَى النَّاسُ بِالْجَارِ مَا شَاءَ اللَّهُ ثُمَّ أُرِيدُ أَنْ أَبِيعَ الطَّعَامَ الْمَضْمُونَ عَلَىَّ إِلَى أَجَلٍ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ لَهُ سَعِيدٌ أَتُرِيدُ أَنْ تُوَفِّيَهُمْ مِنْ تِلْكَ الأَرْزَاقِ الَّتِي ابْتَعْتَ فَقَالَ نَعَمْ ‏.‏ فَنَهَاهُ عَنْ ذَلِكَ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ الأَمْرُ الْمُجْتَمَعُ عَلَيْهِ عِنْدَنَا الَّذِي لاَ اخْتِلاَفَ فِيهِ أَنَّهُ مَنِ اشْتَرَى طَعَامًا بُرًّا أَوْ شَعِيرًا أَوْ سُلْتًا أَوْ ذُرَةً أَوْ دُخْنًا أَوْ شَيْئًا مِنَ الْحُبُوبِ الْقِطْنِيَّةِ أَوْ شَيْئًا مِمَّا يُشْبِهُ الْقِطْنِيَّةَ مِمَّا تَجِبُ فِيهِ الزَّكَاةُ أَوْ شَيْئًا مِنَ الأُدْمِ كُلِّهَا الزَّيْتِ وَالسَّمْنِ وَالْعَسَلِ وَالْخَلِّ وَالْجُبْنِ وَالشَّبْرَقِ وَاللَّبَنِ وَمَا أَشْبَهَ ذَلِكَ مِنَ الأُدْمِ فَإِنَّ الْمُبْتَاعَ لاَ يَبِيعُ شَيْئًا مِنْ ذَلِكَ حَتَّى يَقْبِضَهُ وَيَسْتَوْفِيَهُ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1335" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: Said ibn al-Musayyab (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that Yahya ibn Said heard Jamil ibn Abd ar-Rahman the Muadhdhin say to Said ibn al-Musayyab, &quot;I am a man who buys whatever Allah wills of the receipts for the provisions which people are offered at al-Jar. I want to take payment for goods that I guarantee to deliver at a future date.&quot; Said said to him, &quot;Do you intend to settle these things with receipts for provisions you have bought?&quot; He said, &quot;Yes.&quot; So he forbade that.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1335]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1335" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1335</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1335 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said, &quot;The generally agreed on way of doing things among us in which there is no dispute, about buying food - wheat, barley, durra-sorghum, pearl millet, or any pulse or anything resembling pulses on which zakat is obliged, or condiments of any sort - oil, ghee, honey, vinegar, cheese, sesame oil, milk and so on, is that the buyer should not re- sell any of that until he has taken possession and complete delivery of it</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1335" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

</section>

<h3>Hindi</h3>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1335</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1335 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">जमील बिन अब्दुर्रहमान ने सईद बिन मुसय्यब से कहा मैं इन घलो को जो सर्कार की तरफ से लोगों को मुक़र्रर हैं &#x27;जार&#x27; में खरीद करता हूँ फिर मैं चाहता हूँ के ग़ल्ले को मीयाद लगा कर लोगों के हाथ बेचू. सईद ने कहा तू चाहता है इन लोगों को इसी ग़ल्ले में से ऐडा करे जो तूने खरीदा है? जमील ने कहा हाँ. सईद बिन मुसय्यब ने इस से मना किया. कहा मालिक ने हमारे नज़दीक ये हुकुम इत्तेफ़ाक़ी है; जो शख्स अनाज खरीद करे जैसे गेहूं बाजरा शहद या सिरका या पनीर या दूध या तिल का तेल और जो इसके मुशाबा हैं तोह इन में से कोई चीज़ न बेचे जब तक उन पर कब्जा न कर ले.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1335" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">جمیل بن عبدالرحمن نے سعید بن مسیب سے کہا میں ان غلوں کو جو سرکار کی طرف سے لوگوں کو مقرر ہیں جار میں خرید کرتا ہوں پھر میں چاہتا ہوں کہ غلہ کو میعاد لگا کر لوگوں کے ہاتھ بیچوں سعید نے کہا تو چاہتا ہے ان لوگوں کو اسی غلہ میں سے ادا کرے جو تو نے خریدا ہے جمیل نے کہا ہاں سعید بن مسیب نے اس سے منع کیا ۔ کہا مالک نے ہمارے نزدیک یہ حکم اتفاقی ہے جو شخص اناج خرید کرے جیسے گیہوں جو جوار باجرہ ڈالیں وغیرہ جن میں زکوة واجب ہوتی ہے یا روٹی کے ساتھ کھانے کی چیزیں جیسے زیتون کا تیل یا گھی یا شہد یا سرکہ یا پنیر یا دودھ یا تل کا تیل اور جو اس کے مشابہ ہیں تو ان میں سے کوئی چیز نہ بیچے جب تک ان پر قبضہ نہ کر لے۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1335" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Jameel bin Abdurrahman ne Saeed bin Musayyib se kaha main in ghalon ko jo sarkar ki taraf se logon ko muqarrar hain &#x27;Jaar&#x27; mein khareed karta hoon phir main chahta hoon ke ghalle ko miyaad laga kar logon ke hath bechu. Saeed ne kaha tu chahta hai in logon ko isi ghalle mein se ada kare jo tune khareeda hai? Jameel ne kaha haan. Saeed bin Musayyib ne is se mana kiya. Kaha Malik ne hamare nazdeek ye hukum ittefaaqi hai; jo shakhs anaaj khareed kare jaise gehun, jow, jawar, bajra, dalen wagera jin mein zakat wajib hoti hai ya roti ke sath khane ki cheezein jaise zaitoon ka tel ya ghee ya shahad ya sirka ya paneer ya doodh ya til ka tel aur jo iske mushaba hain toh in mein se koi cheez na beche jab tak un par qabza na kar le.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1335" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 31</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ أَبِي الزِّنَادِ، أَنَّهُ سَمِعَ سَعِيدَ بْنَ الْمُسَيَّبِ، وَسُلَيْمَانَ بْنَ يَسَارٍ، يَنْهَيَانِ أَنْ يَبِيعَ الرَّجُلُ، حِنْطَةً بِذَهَبٍ إِلَى أَجَلٍ ثُمَّ يَشْتَرِيَ بِالذَّهَبِ تَمْرًا قَبْلَ أَنْ يَقْبِضَ الذَّهَبَ</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: Sa&#x27;id ibn al-Musayyab (successor); Sulayman ibn Yasar (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that Abu&#x27;z-Zinad heard Sa&#x27;id ibn al-Musayyab andSulayman ibn Yasar forbid a man to sell wheat for gold on delayed terms and then to buy dried dates with the gold before he had taken delivery of the gold</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1336]</p>

## Narration 1323

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1337)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 31</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1337]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1337" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1337</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1337 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ كَثِيرِ بْنِ فَرْقَدٍ، ‏.‏ أَنَّهُ سَأَلَ أَبَا بَكْرِ بْنَ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ عَمْرِو بْنِ حَزْمٍ عَنِ الرَّجُلِ، يَبِيعُ الطَّعَامَ مِنَ الرَّجُلِ بِذَهَبٍ إِلَى أَجَلٍ ثُمَّ يَشْتَرِي بِالذَّهَبِ تَمْرًا قَبْلَ أَنْ يَقْبِضَ الذَّهَبَ فَكَرِهَ ذَلِكَ وَنَهَى عَنْهُ ‏.‏ وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، بِمِثْلِ ذَلِكَ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَإِنَّمَا نَهَى سَعِيدُ بْنُ الْمُسَيَّبِ وَسُلَيْمَانُ بْنُ يَسَارٍ وَأَبُو بَكْرِ بْنُ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ عَمْرِو بْنِ حَزْمٍ وَابْنُ شِهَابٍ عَنْ أَنْ لاَ يَبِيعَ الرَّجُلُ حِنْطَةً بِذَهَبٍ ثُمَّ يَشْتَرِيَ الرَّجُلُ بِالذَّهَبِ تَمْرًا قَبْلَ أَنْ يَقْبِضَ الذَّهَبَ مِنْ بَيْعِهِ الَّذِي اشْتَرَى مِنْهُ الْحِنْطَةَ فَأَمَّا أَنْ يَشْتَرِيَ بِالذَّهَبِ الَّتِي بَاعَ بِهَا الْحِنْطَةَ إِلَى أَجَلٍ تَمْرًا مِنْ غَيْرِ بَائِعِهِ الَّذِي بَاعَ مِنْهُ الْحِنْطَةَ قَبْلَ أَنْ يَقْبِضَ الذَّهَبَ وَيُحِيلَ الَّذِي اشْتَرَى مِنْهُ التَّمْرَ عَلَى غَرِيمِهِ الَّذِي بَاعَ مِنْهُ الْحِنْطَةَ بِالذَّهَبِ الَّتِي لَهُ عَلَيْهِ فِي ثَمَنِ التَّمْرِ فَلاَ بَأْسَ بِذَلِكَ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَقَدْ سَأَلْتُ عَنْ ذَلِكَ غَيْرَ وَاحِدٍ مِنْ أَهْلِ الْعِلْمِ فَلَمْ يَرَوْا بِهِ بَأْسًا ‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1337" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">This source entry preserves 3 separately attributed reports in their original order. They remain one provider entry, with their distinct narrator introductions and source references. Report type: successor report. Attribution: Abu Bakr ibn Muhammad ibn Amr ibn Hazm (successor). The direct answer, separately introduced abbreviated transmission, and separately attributed joint ruling retain three source groups. Subsequent compiler explanation remains context. Report type: abbreviated transmission. Attribution: Ibn Shihab (successor). The direct answer, separately introduced abbreviated transmission, and separately attributed joint ruling retain three source groups. Subsequent compiler explanation remains context. Report type: successor report. Attribution: Said ibn al-Musayyab (successor); Sulayman ibn Yasar (successor); Abu Bakr ibn Muhammad ibn Amr ibn Hazm (successor); Ibn Shihab (successor). The direct answer, separately introduced abbreviated transmission, and separately attributed joint ruling retain three source groups. Subsequent compiler explanation remains context.</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 1</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that Kathir ibn Farqad asked Abu Bakr ibn Muhammad ibn Amr ibn Hazm about a man who sold food to be delivered at a future date to a man for gold and then with the gold, he bought dates before he had taken delivery of the gold. He disapproved of that and forbade it.</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 2</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Ibn Shihab the like of that.</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 3</p>

<p dir="ltr">Malik said, &#x27;&#x27;Said ibn al-Musayyab, Sulayman ibn Yasar, Abu Bakr ibn Muhammad ibn Amr ibn Hazm, and Ibn Shihab forbade that a man sell wheat for gold and then buy dates with that gold before he had received the gold from the transaction in which he sold the wheat.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1337]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1337" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1337</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1337 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> </p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1337" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="ltr"> </p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1337" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="ltr"> There is no harm for someone to buy dates on delayed terms, on the strength of the gold for which he sold the wheat, from someone other than the person to whom he sold the wheat before taking possession of the gold, and to refer the one from whom he bought the dates to his debtor who bought the wheat, for the gold he is owed for the dates.&quot; Malik said, &quot;I asked more than one of the people of knowledge about that and they did not see any harm in it</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1337" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">कथीर बिन फ़ारक़द ने अबू बक्र बिन मुहम्मद बिन अम्र बिन हज़्म से पूछा कोई शख्स अनाज को सोने के बदले में मियाद लगा कर बेचे फिर क़ब्ल सोना लेने के उसके बदले में खजूर खरीद ले? उन्हों ने कहा ये मकरूह है और मन किया इस से. इब्न शाहब से भी ऐसा ही मरवी है. कहा मालिक ने सईद बिन मुसय्यब साहब ने इस बात से मना किया है के कोई आदमी गेहूं को सोने के बदले में बेचे फिर उस सोने के बदले खजूर खरीद ले उसी शख्स से जिसके हाथ गेहूं बेचे शख्स से खजूर खरीदे सिवाए उस शख्स के जिसके हाथ गेहूं बेचे हैं और खजूर की क़ीमत का हवाले कर दे उस शख्स पर जिसके हाथों गेहूं बेचे हैं तोह दुरुस्त है.</p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">کثیر بن فرقد نے ابوبکر بن محمد بن عمرو بن حزم سے پوچھا کوئی شخص اناج کو سونے کے بدلے میں میعاد لگا کر بیچے پھر قبل سونا لینے کے اس کے بدلے میں کھجور خرید لے انہوں نے کہا یہ مکروہ ہے اور منع کیا اس سے ۔ ابن شہاب سے بھی ایسا ہی مروی ہے ۔ کہا مالک نے سعید بن المسیب اور سلیمان بن یسار ابوبکر بن محمد اور ابن شہاب نے اس بات سے منع کیا ہے کہ کوئی آدمی گیہوں کو سونے کے بدلے میں بیچے پھر اس سونے کے بدلے کھجور خرید لے اسی شخص نے جس کے ہاتھ گیہوں بیچے قبل اس بات کے کہ سونے پر قبضہ کرے اگر اس سونے کے بدلے میں کسی اور شخص سے کھجور خریدے سوائے اس شخص کے جس کے ہاتھ گیہوں بیچے ہیں اور کھجور کی قیمت کا حوالے کر دے اس شخص پر جس کے ہاتھوں گیہوں بیچے ہیں تو درست ہے۔</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Katheer bin Farqad ne Abu Bakr bin Muhammad bin Amr bin Hazm se pucha koi shakhs anaaj ko sone ke badle mein miyaad laga kar beche phir qabl sona lene ke uske badle mein khajoor khareed le? Unhon ne kaha ye makrooh hai aur mana kiya is se. Ibn Shahab se bhi aisa hi marwi hai. Kaha Malik ne Saeed bin Musayyib, Sulaiman bin Yasar, Abu Bakr bin Muhammad aur Ibn Shahab ne is baat se mana kiya hai ke koi aadmi gehun ko sone ke badle mein beche phir us sone ke badle khajoor khareed le usi shakhs se jiske hath gehun beche, qabl is baat ke ke sone par qabza kare. Agar us sone ke badle mein kisi aur shakhs se khajoor khareede siwaye us shakhs ke jiske hath gehun beche hain aur khajoor ki qeemat ka hawale kar de us shakhs par jiske hathon gehun beche hain toh durust hai.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1337" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

## Narration 1324

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1338)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 0</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: The Prophet, explicitly attributed in Yahyas transmission through Malik. Both complete source fields report the prohibition on bidding over a concluded transaction and proposing over another proposal. The source itself supplies the Prophetic attribution.</p>

<p dir="auto">यह्या ने मुझे मालिक से बयान किया के उन्होंने फरमाया के नबी करीम सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम ने इस से मना फरमाया के कोई शख्स किसी के बेचे हुए सौदे पर सौदा करे, और न ही कोई किसी के पैग़ाम-ए-निकाह पर निकाह का पैग़ाम भेजे।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1338]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1338" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="rtl">یحییٰ نے مجھے مالک سے بیان کیا کہ انہوں نے فرمایا کہ نبی کریم صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے اس سے منع فرمایا کہ کوئی شخص کسی کے بیچے ہوئے سودے پر سودا کرے، اور نہ ہی کوئی کسی کے پیغامِ نکاح پر نکاح کا پیغام بھیجے۔</p>

## Narration 1325

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1339)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 31</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: Muhammad, as explicitly attributed in this complete source field. The exact complete source-language field was individually read. The selected span is a locally explicit report or attributed abbreviated report. Independent statements, legal discussion, and explanatory material outside the selected span remain source context. Boundaries apply only to this field; no source wording, canonical identity, or translation equivalence is invented.</p>

<p dir="rtl">وَقَدْ نَهَى رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم عَنْ بَيْعِ الطَّعَامِ قَبْلَ أَنْ يُسْتَوْفَى ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1339]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1339" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1339</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1339 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ لاَ بَأْسَ بِأَنْ يُسَلِّفَ الرَّجُلُ الرَّجُلَ فِي الطَّعَامِ الْمَوْصُوفِ بِسِعْرٍ مَعْلُومٍ إِلَى أَجَلٍ مُسَمًّى مَا لَمْ يَكُنْ فِي زَرْعٍ لَمْ يَبْدُ صَلاَحُهُ أَوْ تَمْرٍ لَمْ يَبْدُ صَلاَحُهُ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ الأَمْرُ عِنْدَنَا فِيمَنْ سَلَّفَ فِي طَعَامٍ بِسِعْرٍ مَعْلُومٍ إِلَى أَجَلٍ مُسَمًّى فَحَلَّ الأَجَلُ فَلَمْ يَجِدِ الْمُبْتَاعُ عِنْدَ الْبَائِعِ وَفَاءً مِمَّا ابْتَاعَ مِنْهُ فَأَقَالَهُ فَإِنَّهُ لاَ يَنْبَغِي لَهُ أَنْ يَأْخُذَ مِنْهُ إِلاَّ وَرِقَهُ أَوْ ذَهَبَهُ أَوِ الثَّمَنَ الَّذِي دَفَعَ إِلَيْهِ بِعَيْنِهِ وَإِنَّهُ لاَ يَشْتَرِي مِنْهُ بِذَلِكَ الثَّمَنِ شَيْئًا حَتَّى يَقْبِضَهُ مِنْهُ وَذَلِكَ أَنَّهُ إِذَا أَخَذَ غَيْرَ الثَّمَنِ الَّذِي دَفَعَ إِلَيْهِ أَوْ صَرَفَهُ فِي سِلْعَةٍ غَيْرِ الطَّعَامِ الَّذِي ابْتَاعَ مِنْهُ فَهُوَ بَيْعُ الطَّعَامِ قَبْلَ أَنْ يُسْتَوْفَى ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ </p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1339" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="rtl"> قَالَ مَالِكٌ فَإِنْ نَدِمَ الْمُشْتَرِي فَقَالَ لِلْبَائِعِ أَقِلْنِي وَأُنْظِرُكَ بِالثَّمَنِ الَّذِي دَفَعْتُ إِلَيْكَ ‏.‏ فَإِنَّ ذَلِكَ لاَ يَصْلُحُ وَأَهْلُ الْعِلْمِ يَنْهَوْنَ عَنْهُ وَذَلِكَ أَنَّهُ لَمَّا حَلَّ الطَّعَامُ لِلْمُشْتَرِي عَلَى الْبَائِعِ أَخَّرَ عَنْهُ حَقَّهُ عَلَى أَنْ يُقِيلَهُ فَكَانَ ذَلِكَ بَيْعَ الطَّعَامِ إِلَى أَجَلٍ قَبْلَ أَنْ يُسْتَوْفَى ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَتَفْسِيرُ ذَلِكَ أَنَّ الْمُشْتَرِيَ حِينَ حَلَّ الأَجَلُ وَكَرِهَ الطَّعَامَ أَخَذَ بِهِ دِينَارًا إِلَى أَجَلٍ وَلَيْسَ ذَلِكَ بِالإِقَالَةِ وَإِنَّمَا الإِقَالَةُ مَا لَمْ يَزْدَدْ فِيهِ الْبَائِعُ وَلاَ الْمُشْتَرِي فَإِذَا وَقَعَتْ فِيهِ الزِّيَادَةُ بِنَسِيئَةٍ إِلَى أَجَلٍ أَوْ بِشَىْءٍ يَزْدَادُهُ أَحَدُهُمَا عَلَى صَاحِبِهِ أَوْ بِشَىْءٍ يَنْتَفِعُ بِهِ أَحَدُهُمَا فَإِنَّ ذَلِكَ لَيْسَ بِالإِقَالَةِ وَإِنَّمَا تَصِيرُ الإِقَالَةُ إِذَا فَعَلاَ ذَلِكَ بَيْعًا وَإِنَّمَا أُرْخِصَ فِي الإِقَالَةِ وَالشِّرْكِ وَالتَّوْلِيَةِ مَا لَمْ يَدْخُلْ شَيْئًا مِنْ ذَلِكَ زِيَادَةٌ أَوْ نُقْصَانٌ أَوْ نَظِرَةٌ فَإِنْ دَخَلَ ذَلِكَ زِيَادَةٌ أَوْ نُقْصَانٌ أَوْ نَظِرَةٌ صَارَ بَيْعًا يُحِلُّهُ مَا يُحِلُّ الْبَيْعَ وَيُحَرِّمُهُ مَا يُحَرِّمُ الْبَيْعَ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ مَنْ سَلَّفَ فِي حِنْطَةٍ شَامِيَّةٍ فَلاَ بَأْسَ أَنْ يَأْخُذَ مَحْمُولَةً بَعْدَ مَحِلِّ الأَجَلِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَكَذَلِكَ مَنْ سَلَّفَ فِي صِنْفٍ مِنَ الأَصْنَافِ فَلاَ بَأْسَ أَنْ يَأْخُذَ خَيْرًا مِمَّا سَلَّفَ فِيهِ أَوْ أَدْنَى بَعْدَ مَحِلِّ الأَجَلِ وَتَفْسِيرُ ذَلِكَ أَنْ يُسَلِّفَ الرَّجُلُ فِي حِنْطَةٍ مَحْمُولَةٍ فَلاَ بَأْسَ أَنْ يَأْخُذَ شَعِيرًا أَوْ شَامِيَّةً وَإِنْ سَلَّفَ فِي تَمْرٍ عَجْوَةٍ فَلاَ بَأْسَ أَنْ يَأْخُذَ صَيْحَانِيًّا أَوْ جَمْعًا وَإِنْ سَلَّفَ فِي زَبِيبٍ أَحْمَرَ فَلاَ بَأْسَ أَنْ يَأْخُذَ أَسْوَدَ إِذَا كَانَ ذَلِكَ كُلُّهُ بَعْدَ مَحِلِّ الأَجَلِ إِذَا كَانَتْ مَكِيلَةُ ذَلِكَ سَوَاءً بِمِثْلِ كَيْلِ مَا سَلَّفَ فِيهِ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1339" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: Muhammad, as explicitly attributed in this complete source field. Exact English field independently read in full. The selected passage is a locally explicit Prophetic report. Surrounding companion, jurist, compiler and explanatory source material remains under Comments. No source wording is altered and no authenticity or translation-equivalence judgment is made.</p>

<p dir="ltr">The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, forbade selling food before getting delivery of it.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1339]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1339" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1339</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1339 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Nafi that Abdullah ibn Umar said that there was no harm in a man making an advance to another man for food, with a set description and price until a set date, as long as it was not in crops, or dates which had not begun to ripen. Malik said, &quot;The way of doing things among us concerning someone who makes an advance for foodstuffs at a known rate until a stated date, and the date arrives and he finds that there is not enough of what he was sold with the seller to fulfill his order, and so he revokes the sale, is that he must only take back the silver, gold, or price which he paid exactly. He does not buy anything else from the man for the same price until he has got back what he paid. That is because if he took something else besides the price which he paid him or exchanged it for goods other than the goods which he bought from him, it would be selling food before getting delivery of it.&quot; Malik said, &quot;</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1339" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said that it was not good if the buyer regretted his purchase and asked the seller to revoke the sale for him and he would not press him immediately for what he had paid. The people of knowledge forbade that. That was because when the food was made ready for the buyer by the seller, the buyer deferred his due from the seller in order that he might revoke the sale for him. That was the sale of food with delayed terms before taking delivery of the food. Malik said, &quot;The explanation of that is that when the date of delivery comes and the buyer dislikes the food, the seller takes by it money to be paid later and so it is not revocation. Revocation is that in which neither the buyer nor the seller is increased. When increase occurs by deferment of payment for a time period, or by anything which increases one of them over the other or anything which gives one of them profit, it is not revocation. When either of them do that, revocation becomes a sale. There is an indulgence for revocation, partnership, and transfer, as long as i ncrease, decrease, or deferment does not come into them. If increase, decrease, or deferment comes into it, it becomes a sale. Whatever makes a sale halal makes it halal and whatever makes a sale haram makes it haram.&quot; Malik said, &quot;If someone pays in advance for Syrian wheat, there is no harm if he takes a load after the term falls due.&quot; Malik said, &quot;It is the same with whoever advances for any kind of thing. There is no harm in him taking better than whatever he has made an advance for or worse than it after the agreed delivery date. The explanation of that is that if, for instance, a man advances for a certain weight of wheat. There is no harm if he decides to take some barley or Syrian wheat. If he has made an advance for good dates, there is no harm if he decides to take poor quality dates. If he paid in advance for red raisins, there is no harm if he takes black ones, when it happens after the agreed delivery date, and when the measure of what he takes is like the measure of what he paid for in advance</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1339" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1339]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1339" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1339</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1339 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुल्लाह बिन उमर (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा कुछ कबाहत नहीं अगर एक मर्द दूसरे मर्द से सलफ़ (एडवांस) करे अनाज में जब उसका वस्फ़ (सिफ़त) बयान कर दे, निर्ख मुक़र्रर करके, मियाद मुअय्यन पर; जब वो सलाम किसी ऐसे खेत में न हो जिसकी बेहतरी का हाल मालूम न हो या ऐसी खजूर में न हो जिसकी बेहतरी का हाल मालूम न हो। कहा मालिक ने हमारे नज़दीक ये हुकुम है जो शख्स सलफ़ करे अनाज में निर्ख मुक़र्रर करके मुद्दत-ए-मुअय्यन पर, तो जब मुद्दत गुज़रे और ख़रीदार बाये के पास वो अनाज न पाए और सलफ़ को मंसूख़ (फ़सख़) करे तो ख़रीदार को चाहिए अपनी चांदी या सोना दिया हो या क़ीमत दी हुई बा-ऐनह फिर ले, ये न करे के उसके बदले में दूसरी चीज़ बाये से ख़रीद ले जब तक अपने सामान पर क़ब्ज़ा न कर ले, क्यूंकि अगर ख़रीदार ने जो क़ीमत दी है उसके सिवा कुछ ले आया उसके बदले में दूसरा अस्बाब ख़रीद ले तो उसने अनाज को क़ब्ल-ए-क़ब्ज़ा के बेचा और रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने इस से मना किया है। कहा मालिक ने अगर मुश्तरी ने बाये से कहा सलफ़ को फ़सख़ कर डाल और सामान वापस करने के लिए मैं तुझको मोहलत देता हूँ तो ये जाइज़ नहीं और अह्ल-ए-इल्म इसको मना करते हैं क्यूंकि जब मियाद गुज़र गई और अनाज बाये के ज़िम्मे वाजिब हो, अब मुश्तरी ने अपने हक़ वसूल करने में देर की इस शर्त से के बाये सलफ़ को फ़सख़ कर डाले तो गोया मुश्तरी ने अपने अनाज को एक मुद्दत पर बेचा क़ब्ल-ए-क़ब्ज़े के। कहा मालिक ने इसकी मिसाल ये है के जब मुद्दत पूरी हुई और ख़रीदार ने अनाज लेना पसंद न किया तो उस अनाज के बदले में कुछ रुपये ठहरा लिए एक मुद्दत पर तो ये इक़ाला नहीं है। इक़ाला वो है जिसमें कमी बेशी बाये या मुश्तरी की तरफ से न हो। अगर इसमें कमी बेशी होगी या कोई मियाद बढ़ जाएगी या कुछ फ़ायदा मुक़र्रर होगा बाये का या मुश्तरी का तो वो इक़ाला-ए-बई समझा जाएगा और इक़ाला और शिरकत और तौलिया जब तक दुरुस्त हैं के कमी बेशी या मियाद न हो, अगर ये चीज़ें होंगी तो वो नई बई समझेंगे। जिन वुजूह से बई दुरुस्त होती है ये भी दुरुस्त होंगी और जिन वुजूह से बई ना-दुरुस्त होती है ये भी ना-दुरुस्त होगी। कहा मालिक ने जो शख्स सलफ़ में उमदा गेहूं ठहराए फिर मियाद गुज़रने के बाद उस से बेहतर या बुरी ले ले तो कुछ कबाहत नहीं बशर्तेके वज़न वही हो जो ठहरा हो, यही हुकुम अंगूर और खजूर में है।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1339" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">عبداللہ بن عمر نے کہا کچھ قباحت نہیں اگر ایک مرد دوسرے مرد سے سلف کرے اناج میں جب اس کا وصف بیان کر دے نرخ مقرر کر کے میعاد معین پر جب وہ سلم کسی ایسے کھیت میں نہ ہو جس کی بہتری کا حال معلوم نہ ہو یا ایسی کھجور میں نہ ہو جس کی بہتری کا حال معلوم نہ ہو ۔ کہا مالک نے ہمارے نزدیک یہ حکم ہے جو شخص سلف کرے اناج میں نرخ مقرر کر کے مدت معین پر تو جب مدت گزرے اور خریدار بائع (بچنے والا) کے پاس وہ اناج نہ پائے اور سلف کو مسخ کرے تو خریدار کو چاہیے اپنی چاندی یا سونا دیا ہو یا قیمت دی ہوئی بعینہ پھیر لے یہ نہ کرے کہ اس کے بدلے میں دوسری چیز بائع (بچنے والا) سے خرید لے جب تک اپنے ثمن پر قبضہ نہ کر لے کیونکہ اگر خریدار نے جو قیمت دی ہے اس کے سوا کچھ لے آیا اس کے بدلے میں دوسرا اسباب خرید لے تو اس نے اناج کو قبل قبضہ کے بیچا اور رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے اس سے منع کیا ہے۔ کہا مالک نے اگر مشتری (خریدنے والا) نے بائع (بچنے والا) سے کہا سلف کو فسخ کر ڈال اور ثمن واپس کرنے کے لئے میں تجھ کو مہلت دیتا ہوں تو یہ جائز نہیں اور اہل علم اس کو منع کرتے ہیں کیونکہ جب میعاد گزر گئی اور اناج بائع (بچنے والا) کے ذمہ واجب ہو اب مشتری (خریدنے والا) نے اپنے حق وصول کرنے میں دیر کی اس شرط سے کہ بائع (بچنے والا) سلم کو فسخ کر ڈالے تو گویا مشتری (خریدنے والا) نے اپنے اناج کو ایک مدت پر بیچا قبل قبضے کے۔ کہا مالک نے اس کی مثال یہ ہے کہ جب مدت پوری ہوئی اور خریدار نے اناج لینا پسند نہ کیا تو اس اناج کے بدلے میں کچھ روپے ٹھہرا لئے ایک مدت پر تو یہ اقالہ نہیں ہے اقالہ وہ ہے جس میں کمی بیشی بائع (بچنے والا) یا مشتری (خریدنے والا) کی طرف سے نہ ہو اگر اس میں کمی بیشی ہوگی یا کوئی میعاد بڑھ جائے گی یا کچھ فائدہ مقرر ہوگا بائع (بچنے والا) کا یا مشتری (خریدنے والا) کا تو وہ اقالہ بیع سمجھا جائے گا اور اقالہ اور شرکت اور تولیہ جب تک درست ہیں کہ کمی بیشی یا میعاد نہ ہو اگر یہ چیزیں ہوں گی تو وہ نئی بیع سمجھیں گے ۔ جن وجوہ سے بیع درست ہوتی ہے یہ بھی درست ہوں گی اور جن وجوہ سے بیع نادرست ہوتی ہے یہ بھی نادرست ہوگی۔ کہا مالک نے جو شخص سلف میں عمدہ گیہوں ٹھہرائے پھر میعاد گزرنے کے بعد اس سے بہتر یا بری لے لے تو کچھ قباحت نہیں بشرطیکہ وزن وہی ہو جو ٹھہرا ہو یہی حکم انگور اور کھجور میں ہے۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1339" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1339]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1339" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Roman Urdu · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1339</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1339 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Abdullah bin Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha kuch qabahath nahi agar ek mard dusre mard se salaf (advance) kare anaaj mein jab uska wasf (sifat) bayan kar de, nirkh muqarrar karke, miyaad muayyan par; jab woh salam kisi aise khet mein na ho jiski behtari ka haal maloom na ho ya aisi khajoor mein na ho jiski behtari ka haal maloom na ho. Kaha Malik ne hamare nazdeek ye hukum hai jo shakhs salaf kare anaaj mein nirkh muqarrar karke muddat-e-muayyan par, toh jab muddat guzre aur khareedar baaye ke paas woh anaaj na paye aur salaf ko mansookh (fasakh) kare toh khareedar ko chahiye apni chandi ya sona diya ho ya qeemat di hui ba-ainah phir le, ye na kare ke uske badle mein dusri cheez baaye se khareed le jab tak apne saman par qabza na kar le, kyunke agar khareedar ne jo qeemat di hai uske siwa kuch le aaya uske badle mein dusra asbaab khareed le toh usne anaaj ko qabl qabza ke becha aur Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne is se mana kiya hai. Kaha Malik ne agar mushtari ne baaye se kaha salaf ko fasakh kar daal aur saman wapis karne ke liye main tujhko mohlat deta hoon toh ye jaiz nahi aur ahl-e-ilm isko mana karte hain kyunke jab miyaad guzar gayi aur anaaj baaye ke zimme wajib ho, ab mushtari ne apne haq wasool karne mein der ki is shart se ke baaye salaf ko fasakh kar dale toh goya mushtari ne apne anaaj ko ek muddat par becha qabl qabze ke. Kaha Malik ne iski misaal ye hai ke jab muddat puri hui aur khareedar ne anaaj lena pasand na kiya toh us anaaj ke badle mein kuch rupaye thahara liye ek muddat par toh ye iqala nahi hai. Iqala woh hai jisme kami beshi baaye ya mushtari ki taraf se na ho. Agar isme kami beshi hogi ya koi miyaad barh jayegi ya kuch faida muqarrar hoga baaye ka ya mushtari ka toh woh iqala-e-bai samjha jayega aur iqala aur shirkat aur tawliya jab tak durust hain ke kami beshi ya miyaad na ho, agar ye cheezein hongi toh woh nayi bai samjhenge. Jin wujuh se bai durust hoti hai ye bhi durust hongi aur jin wujuh se bai nadurust hoti hai ye bhi nadurust hogi. Kaha Malik ne jo shakhs salaf mein umda gehun thahraye phir miyaad guzarne ke baad us se behtar ya buri le le toh kuch qabahath nahi basharteke wazan wahi ho jo thahra ho, yahi hukum angoor aur khajoor mein hai.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1339" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>
